Abstract: Raw-Hermite sensing and collision on a fixed discrete-velocity set can convert a uniform translation into artificial coupling between nominally distinct nonequilibrium orders. We develop a central-Hermite formulation for a D3Q125 kinetic model with order-resolved log-Gaussian relaxation and compare three variants: raw sensing/raw collision (A), central sensing/raw collision (B), and central sensing/central collision (C). In homogeneous translated second-order perturbations, model C preserves third- and fourth-order modal purity to machine precision, whereas A and B develop boost-dependent cross-order content. Across a grid-CFL-boost matrix, model C reduces the post-transport collision frame discrepancy relative to A by 65.342-98.102% (median 81.131%) in the total relative L-infinity measure. Long-time calculations remain positive and conservative to numerical precision, although the accumulated benefit is configuration dependent because transport continually re-injects frame error. A transport study further reveals a clear trade-off: central-Hermite interface reconstruction strongly suppresses the third-order discrepancy but amplifies the fourth-order discrepancy. The fully central-Hermite collision therefore substantially reduces collision-induced cross-order frame discrepancy, while residual dependence remains due to discrete transport and finite velocity-space representation. This moment-space improvement does not by itself establish a comparable reduction in macroscopic Galilean transport error.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值方法在处理不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程时，往往受到CFL条件的限制，时间步长需依赖于空间网格尺寸，限制了计算效率。本文旨在设计一种高阶、无条件稳定的数值格式，摆脱CFL条件的束缚，同时保证高阶精度。

Result: 在无CFL条件（时间步长独立于空间网格尺寸）下，建立了速度的L2和H1范数误差估计以及压力的L2范数误差估计，时间收敛阶可达6阶。数值实验验证了方案的有效性和理论收敛阶。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose and analyze a class of high-order numerical schemes within a fully discrete finite element framework for the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations with no-slip boundary conditions. The temporal discretization employs a kth-order (k=1,...,6) implicit-explicit backward difference formula (IMEX-BDFk), in which the nonlinear convection term is treated explicitly and the linear Stokes part implicitly, whereas the spatial discretization utilizes Taylor-Hood finite elements. We establish the stability and uniform boundedness of the numerical solution. We further establish optimal order error estimates in both space and time without any CFL-type condition, in the sense that the time step is independent of the spatial mesh size. In three dimensions, these include L2- and H1-norm error estimates for the velocity and L2-norm error estimates for the pressure, with temporal convergence rates up to sixth order for all variables. Numerical experiments are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of the scheme and to confirm the theoretical convergence rates.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验一个常见假设：在简单流动中验证有效的代理模型，能否推广到更复杂的流动场景。

Method: 在CMP slurry film（三维）和Karman vortex street（二维）两种瞬态流动上，对比八种代理模型，涵盖全场/潜空间学习、单步/自回归预测，并采用五种物理指标而非简单RMSE进行评估。

Result: 全场单步模型在CMP film上预测壁面剪切应力误差仅3.2%；潜空间自回归DeepONet在KVS上保留了96%的涡脱落能量；点状RMSE在两个场景下均选错模型。

Conclusion: 代理模型的选择应依据目标流动的动态特征（边界驱动vs自维持振荡），验证需用故障模式分解的物理指标，因为胜出的架构和验证方法均不可迁移。

Abstract: A flow surrogate validated on a simple regime is often taken as evidence that the approach will carry to a richer one. We test this assumption on two transient flows under time-varying boundary conditions emulating the process startup: the three-dimensional slurry film in chemical-mechanical planarisation (CMP), a core semiconductor-manufacturing process, and the two-dimensional Karman vortex street (KVS) behind a cylinder. Eight surrogate models are compared on one shared evaluation pipeline, differing in whether they learn the full field or a latent representation, and whether they predict trajectories in one shot or step by step. No single architecture wins both regimes. On the film, a one-shot full-field model reconstructs the process-relevant cumulative wall shear stress to 3.2% relative error. On the wake, a latent autoregressive DeepONet retains 96% of the shedding power that direct and one-shot models damp to almost zero. The deciding axis is the treatment of time. The self-sustained wake requires the phase memory that autoregressive feedback provides, while the boundary-driven film rewards a direct map. Pointwise RMSE picks the wrong model in both regimes, so the evaluation scores five physical questions instead, the field, its structure, invented motion, amplitude, and timing. The trained surrogates answer queries $10^3$ to $10^4$ times faster than the finite-element solver, but the offline cost of the training simulations means they pay off from the first query beyond the training set for CMP and the third for the KVS. The choice of surrogate should follow the dynamical character of the target flow, and its validation should use failure-mode-resolved metrics, since neither the winning architecture nor its validation transfers.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Runge逼近在反问题和学习型数值方法中至关重要，但其本身是病态的，需要显式的定量估计来描述逼近解的增长与外推距离的关系，这在数值分析中不可或缺。

Result: 显式推导了三种圆构型的逼近指数，得到了一般几何设置下的渐近指数，给出了展开系数的范数估计，并构建了谱精度的数值逼近方案。

Conclusion: 所建立的界为反问题正则化方法的实现提供了关键支撑，同时能够构造Helmholtz方程解的谱精度数值逼近，具有重要的理论价值和实际应用意义。

Abstract: Originating from the complex approximation of holomorphic functions, the Runge approximation for elliptic equations has evolved into a fundamental tool for inverse problems and even learning-based numerical methods since its proposition by Lax, Malgrange, with quantitative characterizations further established by Rüland and Salo. It should be remarked here that Runge approximation is ill-posed. In numerical analysis, explicit quantitative estimates are required to characterize the dependence of the approximant's growth on the outward continuation distance of the original solution. This paper investigates the spatial dependent quantitative Runge approximation for the Helmholtz equation using cylindrical harmonics, considering both interior and exterior boundary value problems. We explicitly derive the relevant indices for the three-circle configuration and obtain asymptotic indices for general geometric settings. The derived results provide norm estimates for the expansion coefficients, which are crucial for the implementation of regularization methods. Furthermore, the established bounds enable the construction of spectrally accurate numerical approximations for solutions to the Helmholtz equation.

</details>

*M Nader Alhomsi,Akram Moustafa,Mohammad Al-Saqqa,Jiahong Wu,Xiaoming Zheng*

Main category: math.NA

Motivation: Huang和Shen提出的高阶BDF-IMEX解耦格式在高雷诺数（低粘度）下会失效，误差上界系数包含粘度的负幂次，导致数值解发散或产生伪解。

Method: 在速度更新步中添加对称半正定的谱消失粘度算子（基于方向性Maday-Kaber-Tadmor核），选择性地阻尼高波数欠分辨模态，不增加渐近计算成本，且保持原误差分析结构不变。

Result: 稳定化格式在k=2,3,4阶保持设计精度，而未稳定格式发散；在Re=10^4的扰动Kovasznay流中准确分辨边界层；在Kelvin-Helmholtz不稳定性问题中复现参考积分诊断结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的谱消失粘度稳定化方案能有效挽救高阶BDF-IMEX解耦格式在高雷诺数下的失效问题，提供与粘度无关的 coercive 控制，保持格式的精度和稳定性。

Abstract: Huang and Shen developed a novel class of high-order BDF-IMEX consistent-splitting schemes for the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations, giving the first rigorous stability and error analysis for a fully decoupled splitting scheme of temporal order higher than two. Extending their analysis from unit viscosity to arbitrary viscosity, this work reveals that the error upper bound coefficient contains inverse powers of the viscosity. Our numerical experiments show that the scheme can break down at high Reynolds number. To save the scheme from this failure, we stabilize it by adding to the velocity update a symmetric positive-semidefinite spectral vanishing viscosity operator, built from the directionally applied Maday-Kaber-Tadmor kernel, which selectively damps the high, under-resolved modes at no additional asymptotic cost and leaves the structure of the error analysis intact. We establish stability and error estimates for the stabilized scheme in which the spectral vanishing viscosity provides viscosity-independent coercive control of the high modes. Three two-dimensional tests demonstrate the robustness and accuracy of the stabilized scheme. For a manufactured solution, the stabilized scheme retains its design order for k=2,3,4, whereas the unstabilized scheme diverges. For a perturbed Kovasznay flow, it accurately resolves the boundary layer at Re=10^4 and drives the perturbation back to the steady state, while the unstabilized scheme blows up. For the Kelvin-Helmholtz instability problem, it reproduces the reference integral diagnostics throughout the reliable regime, whereas the unstabilized scheme produces spurious solutions or blows up.

</details>


### [97] [Implicit Tensor-Train Cross Integration of High-Dimensional Nonlinear PDEs via Fiber-Dependency Elimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23814)
*Behzad Ghahremani,Hessam Babaee*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种纤维依赖消除框架，将TT-cross方法从显式时间积分扩展到隐式格式，解决了隐式格式中交叉所选纤维与相邻纤维之间的依赖性问题，实现了封闭的配置系统，适用于线性和非线性高维PDE，数值实验验证了快速收敛和高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TT-cross秩截断方法仅限于显式时间积分，将其扩展到隐式格式时遇到障碍：交叉选择纤维对应的配置方程依赖于不属于未知集的相邻纤维，导致非线性系统不封闭，无法直接应用标准隐式求解器。

Method: 提出一种纤维依赖消除框架，通过交叉插值恒等式将相邻纤维表达为交叉选择纤维的线性组合，从而生成封闭的配置系统，同时保留TT-cross方法的主要优势。该框架可自然结合牛顿迭代和秩自适应，适用于线性和非线性高维PDE。

Result: 数值实验表明依赖消除迭代快速收敛，保持隐式多步格式的时间精度，实现了包含高达$10^{55}$自由度的全阶离散化高维非线性问题的高效隐式积分。

Conclusion: 成功将TT-cross方法推广到隐式时间积分，解决了纤维依赖性问题，为高维张量微分方程的高效数值求解提供了新工具。

Abstract: Tensor-train (TT) representations have emerged as an effective framework for mitigating the curse of dimensionality in the numerical solution of high-dimensional tensor differential equations. Among existing approaches, TT-cross methods are particularly attractive because they require only pointwise evaluations of the governing equations, naturally accommodate arbitrary nonlinearities, and avoid tangent-space projections and the numerical difficulties associated with nearly singular low-rank factors. However, existing TT-cross rank-truncation methods have been restricted to explicit time integration. Extending TT-cross methods to implicit schemes presents an obstacle: the collocation equations associated with the cross-selected fibers depend on neighboring fibers that are not part of the unknown set. Consequently, the resulting nonlinear system is not closed, preventing the direct application of standard implicit solvers. In this work, we introduce a principled fiber-dependency elimination framework that resolves this obstacle by expressing neighboring fibers as linear combinations of the cross-selected fibers through cross interpolation identities. The resulting formulation produces a closed collocation system while preserving the principal advantages of TT-cross methods. The proposed framework applies to both linear and nonlinear high-dimensional partial differential equations and is naturally combined with Newton iterations and rank adaptivity. Numerical experiments demonstrate rapid convergence of the dependency-elimination iterations, preservation of the temporal accuracy of implicit multistep schemes, and efficient implicit integration of high-dimensional nonlinear problems with full-order discretizations containing up to $10^{55}$ degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [98] [Goal-Oriented Error Estimation for Least-Squares Finite Element Methods via Physically Meaningful Adjoint PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23850)
*Yueyao Wu,Shun Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对一阶系统最小二乘有限元方法，提出基于物理PDE伴随而非最小二乘伴随的目标导向误差估计框架，构建了原始-对偶校正泛函，并给出可计算的后验误差界和均衡标记策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最小二乘方法使用的伴随算子由最小二乘公式诱导，缺乏天然的误差估计器；而物理伴随算子拥有自身的通量系统，可构建内生的最小二乘估计器，从而更有效地进行目标导向误差估计。

Method: 基于连续原始方程和伴随方程建立误差恒等式，构建原始-对偶校正泛函；针对含加权Dirichlet边界通量的输出，构造两种校正近似（仅用势函数和同时使用势函数与通量）；利用最小二乘泛函给出可计算的后验界和均衡标记指标。

Result: 推导出乘积型误差估计，最小二乘泛函的和等于两个估计器的精确乘积；数值实验验证了收敛性和标记策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的物理伴随驱动的最小二乘误差估计框架优于传统伴随方法，为FOSLS方法提供了更自然、更高效的目标导向自适应策略。

Abstract: We develop a goal-oriented error-estimation framework for first-order system least-squares (FOSLS) finite element methods based on the physical PDE adjoint rather than the adjoint induced by the least-squares formulation. Identified explicitly from the differential operator, its mixed boundary conditions, and the output, the physical adjoint admits its own first-order flux system and hence a native, built-in least-squares estimator, which the least-squares-induced adjoint does not. Our error identities rest on a primal-dual corrected functional: they follow from the continuous primal and adjoint equations alone, hold for arbitrary conforming approximations, and require no Galerkin orthogonality. For outputs containing a weighted Dirichlet-boundary flux, whose weight becomes the essential datum of the adjoint, we construct two corrected approximations, one from the potentials and one also using the fluxes, and prove product-type error estimates in the primal and adjoint errors. The least-squares functionals then yield computable a posteriori bounds and a balanced marking indicator whose element sum equals the product of the two estimators exactly. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted convergence and the effectiveness of the marking.

</details>


### [99] [Recursive Governance: A Graph-Theoretic Framework for Risk Propagation and Drift Detection in Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23916)
*Sriram Nagaraj,Advaith Nila Narayanan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种Inventory-as-Code（IaC）治理框架，将模型库存视为动态架构组件，通过自主性评分、DAG风险传播、轨迹监控和实际工程问题处理，解决传统模型风险管理在自主智能体系统中的结构性过时问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构从传统预测模型向自主智能体系统过渡，传统模型风险管理（MRM）的静态模型库存要求面临结构性过时，需要新的动态治理框架。

Method: 1) 提出校准的自主度（DoA）重要性评分与工具复杂度加权方案；2) 将智能体库存构建为有向无环图（DAG），定义复合风险传播算法；3) 开发基于集成思维链（CoT）嵌入的分布余弦漂移轨迹监控协议，包括黄金路径基线构建和两阶段响应机制；4) 处理LLM版本变化、潜在反馈循环等实际问题。

Result: 提出了四个主要贡献：DoA评分、DAG风险传播、CoT轨迹监控协议及实际工程问题解决方案；其中匹配自举校准被证明是避免朴素替代方案中严重假阳性伪影的必要条件。

Conclusion: Inventory-as-Code治理循环能够有效应对自主智能体系统带来的模型风险管理挑战，将模型库存从静态文档转变为动态架构组件。

Abstract: As financial institutions transition from traditional predictive models to autonomous agentic systems, the static model inventory requirements of traditional model risk management (MRM) face structural obsolescence. This paper proposes a dynamic Inventory-as-Code (IaC) governance loop that treats the model inventory as a living architectural component rather than a periodic documentation artifact. We make four principal contributions. First, we introduce a calibrated Degree of Autonomy (DoA) materiality score with an explicit, taxonomized tool-complexity weighting scheme that addresses the dominance problem of naive additive risk formulations. Second, we construct the agent inventory as a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) and define a formal Composite Risk Propagation algorithm under which upstream validation failures induce risk penalties on all reachable descendants. Third, we develop a Trajectory Monitoring protocol based on distributional cosine drift across ensembled Chain-of-Thought (CoT) embeddings, with an explicit procedure for constructing and certifying the Golden Path baseline, a matched-bootstrap calibration that we show is necessary to avoid a severe false-positive artifact in the naive alternative, and a two-stage response that separates legitimate reasoning variation from detrimental drift without demanding that a single exceedance event trigger an irreversible action. Fourth, we address practical complications largely absent from the prior literature: LLM base-model version changes, latent feedback loops in nominally acyclic agent graphs, and a two-pass execution structure that stages validation ahead of risk propagation.

</details>


### [100] [A multi-physics structure-preserving integrator with uniform error bounds for relativistic charged-particle dynamics under strong magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24058)
*Mengting Hu,Yifa Tang,Bin Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种显式多物理场保结构Strang分裂格式，用于强磁场中相对论带电粒子四维动力学系统，该格式能保持体积、能量和洛伦兹不变性，并具有二阶一致误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对强磁场中相对论带电粒子的多尺度动力学问题，现有数值格式难以同时保持系统的几何结构（体积、能量、洛伦兹不变性）并保证高精度收敛。

Method: 基于最大排序标度，设计显式多物理场保结构Strang分裂方案，将四维相对论带电粒子动力学系统分解为多个子问题分别处理。

Result: 该格式同时保持体积、能量和洛伦兹不变性，位置和并行速度分量具有二阶一致误差界，理论证明与数值实验一致。

Conclusion: 所提格式在保持物理系统关键几何结构的同时实现了二阶精度，为强磁场相对论带电粒子模拟提供了有效的数值工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop an explicit multi-physics structure-preserving Strang splitting scheme for a four-dimensional relativistic charged-particle dynamical system in the presence of a strong magnetic field under the maximal ordering scaling. The proposed scheme not only preserves volume, energy, and Lorentz invariance, but also yields second-order uniform error bounds for the position and the parallel velocity component. We present rigorous theoretical proofs for these geometric properties and the error convergence, which are subsequently validated by several numerical experiments.

</details>


### [101] [A vectorial lattice Boltzmann scheme for the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24059)
*Denise Aregba-Driollet,Thomas Bellotti,Roberto Natalini,Tommaso Tenna*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Carfora-Natalini离散速度动理论近似的二阶精确向量格子Boltzmann方法，用于不可压Navier-Stokes系统，并通过谱分析和非线性数值验证与松弛方案进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Carfora和Natalini离散速度动理论近似的启发，旨在开发一种二阶精确的向量格子Boltzmann格式，以改善不可压Navier-Stokes系统的求解精度。

Method: 基于Carfora-Natalini的离散速度动理论近似，构建了二阶精确的向量格子Boltzmann格式；在线性化情形下进行谱分析，并在真正非线性问题上进行数值验证，与松弛方案对比优缺点。

Result: 通过谱分析和非线性数值实验，揭示了所提方案相较于松弛方案的优势与不足。

Conclusion: 该向量格子Boltzmann方案在不可压Navier-Stokes系统中具有二阶精度，与松弛方案相比各有优劣，具体表现由谱分析和数值实验共同说明。

Abstract: We introduce a second-order accurate vectorial lattice Boltzmann scheme for the incompressible Navier-Stokes system, inspired by a discrete-velocity kinetic approximation proposed by Carfora and Natalini [ESAIM: M2AN, 42(1), 93-112, 2008]. Advantages and drawbacks compared to relaxation schemes are investigated by providing spectral analyses in the linearized case, and numerical validations on the genuinely non-linear problem.

</details>


### [102] [A note on the discrete Unmapped Tent Pitching for the heterogeneous wave equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24066)
*Marcella Bonazzoli,Gabriele Ciaramella,Ilario Mazzieri*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文讨论了UTP算法在异构设置下的并行求解波类问题，发现离散层面的最优分解策略与连续分析预测的不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构设置下扩展UTP算法，比较不同时空分解的计算成本，以优化并行求解效率。

Method: 在连续层面分析后，进一步在离散层面进行观察和比较，探索最优分解策略。

Result: 离散层面的最优分解策略与连续分析预测的结果不同。

Conclusion: 离散层面分析对于确定实际最优分解策略至关重要，仅依赖连续分析可能得出不同结论。

Abstract: The Unmapped Tent Pitching (UTP) algorithm is a space-time domain decomposition method for the parallel solution of wave-type problems. We have recently extended UTP to heterogeneous settings and compared, at the continuous level, the computational cost of different space-time decompositions. In this note, we report discrete-level observations showing that the optimal decomposition strategy may differ from the one predicted by the continuous analysis.

</details>


### [103] [A convergent Scharfetter-Gummel scheme for a three-species drift-diffusion model for memristors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24086)
*Ansgar Jüngel,Zhiwei Sun,Sara Xhahysa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种用于半导体三物种漂移-扩散模型的结构保持全隐式Scharfetter-Gummel有限体积格式，证明了离散解的存在性、自由能不等式和数值格式的收敛性，并通过二维数值模拟验证了忆阻器中的灯丝形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对忆阻器器件中电子、空穴和氧空位三物种漂移-扩散模型的数值模拟需求，提出一种能够保持物理结构（如自由能耗散）的全隐式有限体积格式。

Method: 采用全隐式Scharfetter-Gummel有限体积格式，将通量重写为迎风形式以揭示隐藏的Fisher信息分量，针对Bernoulli函数的退化特性引入额外的边棱 coercivity 估计，从而建立局部和全局耗散估计。

Result: 建立了离散有限体积解的存在性、离散自由能不等式以及数值格式的收敛性；二维数值模拟验证了格式的结构保持特性，并成功展示了忆阻器中的灯丝形成现象。

Conclusion: 所提出的全隐式Scharfetter-Gummel格式能够有效保持半导体漂移-扩散模型的物理结构，适用于忆阻器器件中灯丝形成过程的数值模拟。

Abstract: A structure-preserving fully implicit Scharfetter-Gummel finite-volume scheme for a three-species drift-diffusion model for semiconductors is proposed and analyzed. The equations describe the evolution of the electron, hole, and oxygen vacancy densities in a (bounded) memristor device, coupled to the Poisson equation for the electric potential, with mixed-type boundary conditions. Recasting the Scharfetter-Gummel fluxes in an upwind form, a hidden Fisher information component is revealed. Owing to the degeneracy of the Bernoulli function appearing in the fluxes, additional edgewise coercivity estimates are required, leading to refined local and global dissipation estimates. Using these ideas, the existence of a discrete finite-volume solution, a discrete free energy inequality, and the convergence of the numerical scheme are established. Numerical simulations in two space dimensions confirm the structure-preserving properties of the scheme and illustrate the filament formation in a memristor device.

</details>


### [104] [Randomized Krylov-Projected Iterated Tikhonov Regularization for Large-Scale Ill-posed Problems Under A Posteriori Stopping Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24138)
*Ravi Verma,Harshit Bajpai,Ankik Kumar Giri*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出两种新的随机迭代正则化框架 RIGKT 和 RIAT，结合随机迭代Tikhonov正则化与Krylov子空间投影技术，用于求解大规模线性病态逆问题，并建立了完整的正则化理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定性正则化方法依赖固定迭代次数，缺乏随机化、Krylov子空间降维与可实施早停的统一理论框架。

Method: 将随机迭代Tikhonov正则化与Krylov子空间投影（Golub-Kahan双对角化用于矩形系统，Arnoldi分解用于方阵系统）相结合，引入随机方程选择、自适应步长策略以及基于差异的全局后验早停准则。

Result: 建立了Bregman距离单调性、有限终止、精确数据收敛、噪声下路径稳定性等正则化性质；证明停止迭代几乎必然且均方收敛到真解；CT和图像去模糊实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 首次为同时包含随机化、Krylov子空间降维和可实施早停的框架提供了严格的正则化理论，数值实验表明在各种噪声水平下均能可靠重建结构特征。

Abstract: We introduce two novel randomized iterative regularization frameworks, termed \texttt{RIGKT} and \texttt{RIAT}, for solving large-scale linear ill-posed inverse problems governed by systems of equations. The proposed methods combine randomized iterated Tikhonov regularization with Krylov subspace projection techniques, utilizing Golub--Kahan bidiagonalization for general rectangular systems (\texttt{RIGKT}) and Arnoldi decomposition for square systems (\texttt{RIAT}). Unlike existing deterministic schemes that rely on fixed iteration counts, our framework incorporates randomized equation selection, an adaptive step-size strategy, and a global, discrepancy-based a posteriori early-stopping rule tailored specifically to the stochastic setting. We present a comprehensive regularization analysis establishing Bregman-distance monotonicity, finite termination, exact-data convergence, and pathwise stability under noise. Furthermore, we prove that the stopped iterates converge almost surely and in the mean-square sense to the true solution, establishing a rigorous regularization property. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first theoretical framework to simultaneously account for randomization, Krylov-subspace dimension reduction, and implementable early stopping. Numerical experiments involving two-dimensional X-ray computed tomography (CT) and image deblurring demonstrate that \texttt{RIGKT} and \texttt{RIAT} reliably reconstruct structural features across various noise regimes.

</details>


### [105] [Convergence of a scheme for a one dimensional nonlocal and nonlinear eikonal equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24216)
*Diana Al Zareef,Ahmad El Hajj,Antoine Zurek*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维非局部非线性eikonal方程，提出了半显式(IMEX)数值格式，证明了熵保持性和黏性收敛性，并通过数值实验验证了模型和算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对空间梯度无符号限制的非局部非线性eikonal方程研究不足，该方程在弱正则性假设下缺乏有效的数值方法。

Method: 推导了该模型的周期形式，提出了半显式(IMEX)数值格式，从理论上证明了该格式保持离散梯度熵估计并具有黏性意义下的收敛性。

Result: 所提出的IMEX格式能够保持离散梯度熵估计，且在黏性意义下收敛，数值实验展示了模型行为和算法性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为非局部eikonal方程提供了一种有效的数值求解方案，为后续相关研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this work, we study a one-dimensional nonlocal and nonlinear eikonal equation without sign restriction on its spatial gradient. The equation is characterized by weak regularity assumptions on both the nonlocal velocity field and the initial data. We derive a periodic version of this model and propose a semi-explicit (IMEX) scheme for its numerical approximation. We prove that the scheme preserves a discrete gradient entropy estimate and establish its convergence in the viscosity sense. Finally, we present numerical results illustrating the behavior of the model and the performance of the proposed scheme.

</details>


### [106] [The $L_1$-Discrepancy with Nonnegative Weights Suffers from the Curse of Dimensionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24290)
*Josef Dick*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有非负权重的L1-差异度存在维度诅咒，即所需的点集大小随维度指数增长，下界为(1.07735)^d。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究L1-差异度在任意非负权重下是否受维度诅咒影响，这是拟蒙特卡洛方法中的基本问题。

Method: 结合体积偏置概率测度的变化和归一化差异函数的分数矩估计。

Result: 证明了对于任意ε∈(0,1)和d∈N，L1-差异度的逆满足N_{1,+}(ε,d) ≥ ((1-ε)^2/(1+ε))·((3+2√3)/6)^d。

Conclusion: 非负权重的L1-差异度确实存在维度诅咒，且证明依赖于权重的非负性，不适用于带符号的权重。

Abstract: We prove that the $L_1$-discrepancy with arbitrary nonnegative weights suffers from the curse of dimensionality. More precisely, for every $\varepsilon \in (0,1)$ and $d \in \mathbb{N}$, the inverse of the $L_1$-discrepancy satisfies \[ N_{1,+}(\varepsilon, d) \ge \frac{(1-\varepsilon)^2}{1 + \varepsilon} \left( \frac{3+2 \sqrt{3}}{6}\right)^d, \] where $(3+2\sqrt{3})/6 = 1.07735\ldots$. The proof combines a change to a volume-biased probability measure with a fractional-moment estimate for the normalized discrepancy function. The lower bound applies, in particular, to equally weighted point sets. The argument uses the nonnegativity of the weights in an essential way and does not cover arbitrary signed weights.

</details>


### [107] [The Convergence and Error Analysis of Coordinate Descent Methods with Compression for Full Configuration Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24319)
*Yingzhou Li,Qiang Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了压缩在坐标下降全组态相互作用（CDFCI）方法中的效果，通过理论分析和数值实验证明了压缩迭代的线性收敛性，并揭示了特征值误差与压缩阈值之间的二次关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全组态相互作用（FCI）基态问题的无约束优化框架下，探讨压缩技术对CDFCI算法的影响，旨在降低计算成本的同时保持收敛性能。

Method: 基于无约束优化形式的CDFCI方法，引入压缩策略，在局部假设下证明压缩迭代线性收敛到受限问题的解，并利用指数衰减假设推导特征值误差的阶数。

Result: 压缩迭代线性收敛到受限问题的解；收敛点被精确刻画；在目标特征向量满足指数衰减假设时，特征值误差为O(τ²)，其中τ为压缩阈值。

Conclusion: 理论分析和数值实验共同验证了压缩CDFCI方法的有效性和理论结果的正确性。

Abstract: We study the effect of compression in Coordinate Descent Full Configuration Interaction (CDFCI) within an unconstrained optimization formulation of the full configuration interaction ground-state problem. Under suitable local assumptions, we prove that the compressed iteration converges linearly to the solution of an associated restricted problem. We also characterize the convergence point of the compressed algorithm. Under an additional exponential decay assumption on the target eigenvector, we show that the resulting eigenvalue error is of order $τ^2$, where $τ$ denotes the compression threshold. Numerical results support the analysis.

</details>


### [108] [Fifth-order finite volume derivative-based Hermite WENO scheme with unified stencils for hyperbolic conservation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24325)
*Peiwen Chen,Zhuang Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于导数的有限体积Hermite WENO格式用于双曲守恒律，通过排除目标单元导数信息并使用统一模板进行空间重构，在保持高精度的同时增强了鲁棒性并降低了计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HWENO格式在处理间断时，导数可能变得过大影响鲁棒性；为减少导数影响而采用不同模板分别重构控制方程和导数方程，导致计算成本高且算法复杂。本文旨在克服这些局限。

Method: 在空间重构中排除目标单元导数信息，同时在时间演化中使用相同重构多项式来限制导数；采用统一模板而非分离模板；支持任意正线性权重且权重之和为1，保持紧致模板。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提格式具有高阶精度、高效性、高分辨率和良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于导数的HWENO方案通过统一模板和排除目标单元导数信息，在保持精度的同时提升了鲁棒性并降低了计算复杂度和算法实现难度。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a derivative-based finite volume Hermite WENO (HWENO) scheme for hyperbolic conservation laws, where both the solution and its first-order derivatives are evolved in time and utilized in spatial reconstructions. The key challenge for solving hyperbolic conservation laws is the possible emergence of discontinuities in the numerical solutions. When facing discontinuities, the derivatives can become excessively large, which may compromise the robustness of HWENO schemes. In the first HWENO scheme, different sets of stencils were adopted for reconstructing the governing equation and the derivative equation, respectively, aiming to reduce the influence of the derivatives while preserving high-order accuracy. However, this approach not only substantially increases computational cost but also introduces considerable algorithmic complexity. To overcome these limitations, we exclude the information of the target cell's derivatives from spatial reconstructions, while employing the same reconstructed polynomial during temporal evolution to limit the derivatives. This strategy enhances the robustness of traditional HWENO schemes and allows unified stencils within the derivative-based HWENO framework. Furthermore, the proposed scheme supports arbitrary positive linear weights that sum to one and maintains a compact stencil. Numerical results demonstrate the high-order accuracy, efficiency, high resolution, and robustness of the proposed HWENO scheme.

</details>


### [109] [A Curve-Reference Exponential Integrator for Three-Dimensional Charged-Particle Dynamics under a Strong Nonconstant Magnetic Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24523)
*Zhirui Shen,Bin Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对非相对论动量形式的三维带电粒子动力学模型，提出了一种曲线参考指数积分器（CREI），解决了传统方法无法处理线性部分零特征值非半单的问题，并通过核-值域分解和沿精确振荡曲线展开非线性项，实现了均匀误差界和数值收敛性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统局部线性化扩展指数积分器（LLEEI）要求系统满足特定谱条件，但该模型线性部分的零特征值非半单，导致经典方法失效，亟需构造新的数值格式。

Method: 对主磁场矩阵进行核-值域分解，并通过线性变换将方程转化为具有半单零块和常值反对称振荡块的系统；在此基础上沿精确振荡曲线而非固定点展开非线性项，保留至k次单项式构造CREI(k+1)。

Result: 证明了均匀的局部和全局误差界，并通过可复现的数值实验验证了关于步长h的收敛性和关于参数ε的一致性。

Conclusion: CREI方法有效解决了非半单纯性带来的数值困难，为带电粒子动力学模拟提供了一种精度高、误差均匀可控的新型数值积分器。

Abstract: We study a three-dimensional charged-particle dynamics model in the nonrelativistic momentum formulation and construct a curve-reference exponential integrator (CREI) for this system. Since the zero eigenvalue of linear section is not semisimple, the system after usual change of variables does not meet the spectral condition used in the classical locally linearized extended exponential integrator (LLEEI). A kernel-range decomposition of the dominant magnetic matrix and a further linear transformation reduce the equation to a system with a semisimple zero block and one constant skew-symmetric oscillatory block. The CREI expands the nonlinear term along the exact oscillatory curve rather than at a fixed point. Retaining monomials through degree k defines CREI(k + 1). We prove uniform local and global error bounds and present reproducible numerical experiments for convergence in h and uniformity in ε.

</details>


### [110] [Well-conditioned Electric Field Surface Integral Equations using Reflective Generalized Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24526)
*Yossi Dahan,Suryakumar Sivakumar,Yaniv Brick,Simon B. Adrian*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出广义源积分方程（GSIE）方法，通过向传统电场积分方程（EFIE）添加辅助贡献来消除内部共振，无需组合公式。研究表明，使用内部散射凸面屏蔽产生的辅助核，GSIE算子可避免内部本征和准共振，且具有良好谱特性和可压缩性，适用于快速迭代求解器设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统电场积分方程（EFIE）在内部共振频率处出现的病态问题，避免使用组合公式（如CFIE）带来的额外复杂性和计算成本。

Method: 采用广义源方法，通过增强传统EFIE核的辅助贡献来构造GSIE算子；利用同心圆形散射体和屏蔽的闭式表达式进行分析；采用Calderón型预处理和GSIE-dual/Yukawa核预条件器。

Result: GSIE算子（TM和TE模式）在适当屏蔽参数下无内部本征和准共振；辅助分量是EFIE的紧扰动，继承了密集离散化崩溃但可被Calderón预处理治愈；高频崩溃机制受辅助分量影响，部分情况下更具韧性；非圆形屏蔽下结论依然有效；GSIE-dual和Yukawa核预条件器具有良好谱特性和可压缩性。

Conclusion: 二维屏蔽基GSIE算子为扩展到三维问题和更广泛几何形状的辅助核奠定了基础，为快速迭代求解器设计提供了有吸引力的方案。

Abstract: This work uses the generalized source approach to develop a class of well-conditioned integral operators that are free of internal resonance, without the need for combined formulations. The Generalized source integral equations (GSIEs) kernels are obtained by augmenting the conventional electric field integral equation (EFIE) kernel with auxiliary contributions to enhance the rank deficiency of the corresponding moment matrix blocks. This paper presents the first investigation of the spectra of GSIE operators for auxiliary kernels produced by internal scattering convex shields. Using closed-form expressions for concentric circular scatterers and shields, it is shown that, with shield parameters that are suitable for enhanced compressibility, the transverse magnetic (TM)- and transverse electric (TE)-GSIE operators are free of internal proper and quasi-resonances. The auxiliary components are shown to be compact perturbations of their EFIE counterparts. Hence these GSIESs inherit their dense-discretization breakdown, which remains curable via Calderón-type preconditioning. The mechanisms that govern the high-frequency breakdown are shown to be influenced by the auxiliary component, leading, in some cases, to greater resilience. These observations are shown to remain valid for moment matrices and GSIEs designed with non-circular stencil shields. For both GSIE-dual and Yukawa-kernel preconditioners, the formulations exhibit favorable spectral properties while maintaining their compressibility. This makes the formulations attractive for the design of fast iterative solvers. The results on the two-dimensional shield-based operators provide a foundation for extending the approach to three-dimensional problems and to auxiliary kernels with broader geometric applicability.

</details>


### [111] [Arbitrary-Order Padé-Closed Anchored Two-Derivative Time Discretizations: $s$ Active Stages, Order $2s$, and $L$-Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24592)
*Zhixin Huo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一类任意阶隐式双导数单步方法，通过锚定活动阶段公式，利用Hermite矩条件和Padé近似实现L-稳定和高精度，数值验证表明其在宽步长范围内兼具高阶精度和强刚性阻尼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造高阶且L-稳定的数值方法，以有效求解刚性问题，同时避免增加未知阶段状态的数量。

Method: 采用锚定活动阶段公式，每个信息节点同时使用向量场及其一阶全时间导数，通过2s个Hermite矩条件达到全局阶2s，并用第二子对角Padé近似固定剩余系数，再通过Padé-Hermite基定理保证封闭性和稳定性。

Result: 方法具有L-稳定性（从而A-稳定性），符号验证至s=6，高精度计算确认前四个成员的阶数分别为2、4、6、8，与Gauss-Legendre和Radau IIA方法比较显示优势。

Conclusion: 所构造方法在相同活动阶段数下，兼具高阶精度和强刚性阻尼，优于经典方法。

Abstract: An arbitrary-order family of implicit two-derivative one-step methods is constructed in an anchored active-stage formulation. At each information node the method uses both the vector field and its first total time derivative, enriching the local Hermite data without increasing the number of unknown stage states. With the known initial value retained as an anchor and $s$ unknown active stages, $2s$ Hermite moment conditions yield global order $2s$. The two remaining coefficients in each stage row are fixed by the second-subdiagonal Padé approximant $[s-1/s+1]_{e^z}$. For every ordered real node set, a Padé--Hermite basis theorem proves that the closure is unique, preserves all moment conditions, and gives $\det(I-zA-z^2\widehat A)=Q_s(z)$ and $R_s(z)=P_s(z)/Q_s(z)$. Hence the coupled stage system has no hidden poles and the accepted one-step map is $L$-stable (and therefore $A$-stable) for every positive integer $s$. Exact symbolic verification is reported through $s=6$, and high-precision computations confirm orders $2,4,6,$ and $8$ for the first four members. At equal active-stage count, comparisons with Gauss--Legendre and Radau IIA methods demonstrate the combined high-order accuracy and strong stiff damping of the construction over broad step-size ranges.

</details>


### [112] [Adaptive Multiphysics Coupling for Hyperbolic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24600)
*Simon Candelaresi,Erik Faulhaber,Michael Schlottke-Lakemper*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对不连续伽辽金代码Trixi.jl，实现了自适应耦合任意多个不同物理系统域的能力，通过边界信息交换和用户自定义耦合函数，显著降低计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理多物理场耦合时，往往假设系统间共享变量或变量数目相近，难以处理变量差异大的层次化系统，且缺乏自适应切换模型的能力，导致计算资源浪费。

Method: 在Trixi.jl框架中，通过边界信息交换和用户自定义耦合函数实现不同物理系统的耦合；支持在仿真过程中自适应选择模型，用户可自定义切换准则。

Result: 相比在整个仿真域中运行最复杂模型，所提出的高低复杂度耦合系统方法显著降低了计算时间，且额外开销极小。

Conclusion: 本文提出的自适应多域耦合方法有效解决了不同物理系统间的耦合难题，实现了计算效率的大幅提升，尤其适用于天体物理学等高度动态场景。

Abstract: For the discontinuous Galerkin code Trixi$.$jl we implement capabilities for adaptively coupling arbitrarily many domains with different physics. The coupling of the systems is realized through the exchange of boundary information and user-definable coupling functions. This gives us the ability to couple systems that do not share a single variable or have a very different number of variables. This is particularly useful when we have a hierarchy of systems. Our implementation is such that we can adaptively select the model of the coupled systems in the course of the simulation. This allows for highly dynamical scenarios as can be found e.g. in astrophysics. The criteria for adaptivity can be user defined and tailored to the physical problem. Compared to computing the most complex model in the entire simulation domain, our method of coupled systems of high and low complexity leads to a significant reduction in computational time with very little overhead.

</details>


### [113] [Anderson acceleration of the proximal point method: the exact adaptive minimax, a spectral phase transition, and optimal safeguarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24643)
*Zheng Jia,Yekini Shehu,Yonghong Yao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文严格分析了带残差多项式加速的邻近点方法（PPM），以Anderson加速（AA）为典型自适应方案。回答了三个精确问题：(i) 所有自适应方法的最小最大复杂度精确为每K次预解评估的d₀/(K+1)，最优多项式唯一为Fejér核；(ii) 发现了尖锐的相变：当谱底s满足sK→∞时，Jackson核多项式达到O(d₀/(K²s))，临界尺度s≍1/K时屏障精确为1/(K+1)；(iii) 线性问题AA-PPM无需保护，非线性问题认证O(1/k)包络线每迭代需要恰好两次预解评估且该因子最优。同时修正并完善了结构化问题的理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对带残差多项式加速的邻近点方法进行精确的复杂度分析，特别是以Anderson加速为代表的自适应方案，填补自适应方法最小最大复杂度的理论空白，并揭示谱特性与算法性能之间的精确关系。

Method: 通过构造显式的偏斜自伴实例（特征值位于u^{K+1}=-1的根上，质量服从csc²分布）建立匹配的下界；利用Fejér核、Jackson核等经典多项式分析不同谱尺度下的最优收敛率；对线性和非线性问题分别分析AA-PPM的预解评估需求。

Result: (i) 所有adaptive方法的最小最大复杂度为d₀/(K+1)，由平均反射估计量达到，匹配下界为1/(K+1)；(ii) 谱深度s满足sK→∞时收敛率为O(d₀/(K²s))，s≍1/K时精确屏障为1/(K+1)；(iii) 线性问题AA-PPM无需保护，非线性问题需要每迭代2次预解评估且该因子最优。

Conclusion: 为残差多项式加速PPM建立了精确的复杂度理论，包括自适应方法的最优性、相变现象和预解评估成本的最优性，修正并完善了结构化问题的理论框架。

Abstract: \noindent We study residual-polynomial acceleration of the proximal point method (PPM) for maximal monotone inclusions, with Anderson acceleration (AA) as the prototypical adaptive scheme. We answer three questions exactly. (i)~The minimax complexity over all adaptive methods is precisely $d_0/(K+1)$ per $K$ resolvent evaluations. The upper bound is attained by the averaged-reflection estimator; the matching lower bound uses an explicit skew-adjoint instance with resolvent eigenvalues at the roots of $u^{K+1}=-1$ and $\csc^2$-distributed masses, on which every degree-$K$ polynomial method satisfies $\|r(y_K)\|\ge 1/(K+1)$. The optimal polynomial is uniquely the Fejér kernel, and the same instance certifies a per-step floor. (ii)~A sharp phase transition separates regimes: Jackson-kernel polynomials achieve $O(d_0/(K^2 s))$ when the spectral floor $s$ satisfies $sK\to\infty$, while at the critical scale $s\asymp 1/K$ the barrier is exactly $1/(K+1)$. The picture extends to normal operators and the nonlinear family $M=S+N_C$. (iii)~On linear problems AA-PPM needs no safeguarding; on nonlinear problems certification of the $O(1/k)$ envelope requires exactly two oracle evaluations per iteration, and this factor is optimal. We also correct and complete the theory for structured problems---affine, strongly monotone, piecewise-affine, and Hölderian growth---and confirm all predictions numerically.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

### [114] [Quickest Detection with Rough Path Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22958)
*Mingrui Wang,Prakash Chakraborty*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于粗糙路径的分布变化快速检测框架，将变化前后信号建模为两个独立粗糙路径的拼接，通过最优停止理论求解，检测规则为粗糙路径签名线性泛函首次到达半空间的时间，并扩展至分布鲁棒情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有快速检测方法多假设信号具有良好结构（如马尔可夫性），难以处理复杂、不规则路径信号。粗糙路径理论为描述复杂路径提供了自然工具，但尚未被用于变化检测问题。

Method: 将变化前后动态建模为独立粗糙路径，观测信号为其在未知随机变点处的拼接；将快速检测转化为粗糙路径框架下的最优停止问题；证明最优停止规则为粗糙路径签名线性泛函首次到达半空间的时间；扩展至分布鲁棒版本；采用零阶随机逼近在截断签名系数上实现。

Result: 在布朗运动设定下性能与最优方法相当；在分数布朗运动设定下优于现有方法且对对抗性路径扰动具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了分布变化的快速检测问题，具有几何可解释性和鲁棒性，可推广至模型不确定情形。

Abstract: We develop a framework for quickest detection of distributional change in signals modeled as rough paths. By representing the pre-change and post-change dynamics as two independent rough paths and modeling the observed signal as their concatenation at an unknown random change-point, we formulate quickest detection as an optimal stopping problem using rough paths. We show that the optimal stopping rule takes the form of the first hitting time to a half-space by a linear functional of the rough path signature, and establish that the same structural form arises independently from the intrinsic geometry of the observed path. Statistical guarantees on detection delay and false alarm probability are derived, and the framework is extended to a distributionally robust formulation in which both the pre-change and post-change models are uncertain. The proposed rules are implemented via zeroth-order stochastic approximation over truncated-signature coefficients and evaluated numerically under Brownian and fractional Brownian dynamics, achieving performance comparable to optimal methods in the Brownian setting while outperforming them and remaining robust to adversarial path perturbations in the fractional Brownian setting.

</details>


### [115] [On the Order-Conditional Optimality of Gaffke's Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22971)
*George Bissias,Erik Learned-Miller*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了随机向量概率律标量参数的下置信界（LCB）问题，将Buehler等人的经典工作以纯概率术语重新表述，建立了一个更易理解且可扩展的框架。在独立成分假设下，证明了Gaffke界对于由最大边际均值参数所诱导的序是Buehler最优的，即没有其他有效的LCB能在此基础上改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视下置信界（LCB）问题，将经典工作以更易理解和可扩展的纯概率框架重新表述，并证明Gaffke界在特定序下的最优性。

Method: 将Buehler等人的经典工作以纯概率术语重新表述，构建一个更易理解和可扩展的框架；然后在独立成分假设下，针对最大边际均值参数，证明Gaffke界是Buehler最优的。

Result: 证明了在独立成分假设下，Gaffke界对于由最大边际均值参数所诱导的序是Buehler最优的，即没有其他按照相同方式排序样本的有效LCB能在此基础上改进。

Conclusion: 重新表述的框架更易理解和扩展，且Gaffke界在独立成分假设下对最大边际均值参数具有Buehler最优性。

Abstract: Let $X = (X_1, \ldots, X_n)$ be a random vector from any Borel probability law on $\mathbb{R}_+^n$. We revisit the problem of deriving a lower confidence bound (LCB) on a scalar parameter of that law. We recast classical work, beginning with Buehler, in purely probabilistic terms to form a more accessible and extensible framework. We then specialize the framework to the case where the components of $X$ are independent. In this context, we prove that Gaffke's bound is Buehler optimal for the order that it induces with respect to the maximum marginal mean parameter: $max_{i \in [n]} E_Q[X_i]$, which reduces to the common mean when the $X_i$ are independent and identically distributed. That is to say, no other valid LCB that orders samples in the same way as Gaffke's bound can improve on it with respect to this parameter.

</details>


### [116] [Identifiability and Information-Based Inference for Epidemic Transmission Models Under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23079)
*Md Asaduzzaman*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对动态接触网络上的流行病传播模型，建立了关于参数可识别性与Fisher信息的统一理论框架，揭示了观测设计、网络覆盖与测量精度对统计推断精度的影响，并通过仿真验证了理论预测与有限样本表现的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部分观测流行病传播模型的似然公式虽已建立，但对观测机制下统计推断的理论极限（如可识别性、信息损失）缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 构建统一框架研究结构可识别性与局部可识别性条件，推导观测数据与完整数据的Fisher信息矩阵，通过缺失信息分解量化未观测传播事件和网络信息缺失带来的信息损失。

Result: 建立了观测频率、网络覆盖率和测量精度对参数可估计性与统计效率的影响关系，仿真表明理论预测与有限样本性能高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该框架阐明了观测设计、可识别性与推断精度的内在联系，为部分观测流行病传播模型的统计推断提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Inference for epidemic transmission on dynamic networks is fundamentally limited by latent infection times, incomplete contact histories, imperfect observation, and external sources of infection. Although coherent likelihood formulations are available for partially observed epidemic processes, considerably less is known about the theoretical limits of statistical inference under such observation mechanisms. This paper develops a unified framework for studying identifiability and Fisher information in epidemic transmission models observed on dynamic contact networks. We establish conditions for structural and local identifiability, derive observed and complete-data information matrices, and quantify information loss arising from unobserved transmission events and missing network information through a missing-information decomposition. We further investigate how observation frequency, network coverage, and measurement accuracy influence parameter estimability and statistical efficiency, providing a principled basis for evaluating surveillance strategies. Simulation studies demonstrate that the proposed framework accurately characterises the relationship between observation design, statistical information, and parameter estimation, with theoretical predictions closely matching finite-sample performance. The proposed framework clarifies the relationship between observation design, identifiability, and inferential precision, and provides a theoretical foundation for statistical inference in partially observed epidemic transmission models.

</details>


### [117] [Estimation of multiple precision matrices under shared support with heterogeneous edge strengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23577)
*Sayan Ranjan Bhowal,Debashis Paul,Gopal K Basak,Samarjit Das*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出Multiplicative Graphical Lasso (Mglasso)方法，用于在共享稀疏约束下联合估计多个高维精度矩阵。每个精度矩阵分解为共享结构矩阵和群体特异性矩阵的Schur-Hadamard乘积，通过ADMM算法优化，并提供了高维一致性理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个数据集共享相同的条件依赖结构但具有不同的交互强度时，现有方法难以有效联合估计精度矩阵。传统方法未能区分共享结构与群体特异性变化。

Method: 将每个精度矩阵分解为共享结构矩阵Θ与群体特异性矩阵Γ_l的Schur-Hadamard乘积，使用ℓ₁惩罚实现共享稀疏性，Frobenius范数惩罚控制群体变异，采用ADMM结合梯度下降进行优化。

Result: 理论上证明了目标函数的局部严格凸性及高维一致性（包括上确界范数误差界和精确支撑恢复）。模拟实验表明，在较小样本量下Mglasso的模型选择一致性优于Group Graphical Lasso (GGL)。

Conclusion: Mglasso能够有效处理多个群体间共享条件依赖但边强度不同的高维精度矩阵估计问题，具有良好的理论保障和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Estimating multiple precision matrices in high-dimension presents significant challenges, particularly when distinct datasets share a common conditional dependency structure but exhibit population-specific interaction strengths. We address this problem by introducing the Multiplicative Graphical Lasso (Mglasso), a method for jointly estimating precision matrices across multiple Gaussian graphical models under a shared sparsity constraint. Each precision matrix is decomposed as a Schur-Hadamard product of a shared structural matrix $\boldsymbolΘ$, which encodes the common conditional independence graph, and a population-specific matrix $\boldsymbolΓ_{l}$, which captures variation in edge strengths across populations. We optimize a penalized log-likelihood that utilizes an $\ell_1$-penalty to enforce common sparsity and a Frobenius norm penalty to regulate population-specific variations. The optimization is efficiently performed using the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) algorithm integrated with gradient descent. Theoretically, we establish the local strict convexity of the objective function and provide rigorous high-dimensional consistency guarantees, including supremum norm error bounds and exact support recovery under sub-Gaussian tail conditions. Extensive simulations show superior model selection consistency at smaller sample sizes compared to the benchmark Group Graphical Lasso (GGL). Finally, the method's practical utility is further validated through real-world applications.

</details>


### [118] [Exact Generalization Error Curves of Kernel Ridge Regression for Functional Moment Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23666)
*Yinan Ding,Yicheng Li*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了张量积核岭回归（KRR）在函数型数据中估计r阶矩函数的误差结构，得到了L²误差的精确展开式，并揭示了KRR在不同光滑度和稀疏性下的最优性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管核岭回归是函数型数据分析的标准方法，但其精确行为尚不清晰，尤其是在高阶矩估计和误差结构方面缺乏深入理解。

Method: 采用张量积核岭回归框架，统一处理均值、协方差及高阶矩估计问题；利用U-统计量的浓度不等式，推导出L²误差的1+o_P(1)精确展开式。

Result: 误差展开包含一个偏置项和三个方差项（分别对应样本路径间变异、潜信号变异和测量误差变异）；KRR在源光滑度s≤2时达到最优速率，但在s>2的稀疏场景下由于饱和效应而次优。

Conclusion: 该研究精细揭示了函数型数据中KRR的误差结构，明确了其最优性边界，并为后续方法改进提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Kernel ridge regression is a standard method for functional data analysis, but its exact behavior is less understood. We study tensor-product kernel ridge regression for estimating the $r$-th moment function of a random function based on noisy discrete observations. The formulation includes mean estimation, covariance estimation, and higher-order moment estimation in a single framework. Our main result gives a precise $1+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)$ expansion for the $L^2$ error at each admissible regularization parameter. The expansion consists of bias and three variance terms corresponding respectively to variation across the independent sample paths, latent signal variation at each sample point, and variation from measurement errors, identifying the refined error structure underlying functional data. As applications, we show that KRR attains the minimax rate for source smoothness $s \leq 2$ but becomes suboptimal in the sparse regime for $s>2$ due to saturation. A technical ingredient is a set of concentration inequalities for $U$-statistics suited to the dependent product structure of functional observations.

</details>


### [119] [Proper Bayes minimax multiple shrinkage estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23717)
*Pankaj Bhagwat,William E. Strawderman,Edward I. George*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文首次证明了在平方损失下估计多元正态均值时，存在适当的贝叶斯极小极大多重收缩估计量，通过引入平方根超调和边际混合的通用构建方法，实现了对多个预设目标的适应性收缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有极小极大收缩估计量只能向单个预设目标收缩，而实践中常面临多个候选收缩目标的选择困难，亟需一种能自适应选择更优目标、同时保持极小极大性质的估计方法。

Method: 通过混合平方根超调和边际分布（而非传统的超调和边际），构建适当的贝叶斯极小极大多重收缩估计量；以经典Strawderman收缩估计量的自适应凸组合为例进行说明。

Result: 首次证明了适当的贝叶斯极小极大多重收缩估计量的存在性，该类估计量能自适应地向多个预设目标中更优者收缩，显著扩大了潜在风险降低的区域，同时始终不劣于极大似然估计。

Conclusion: 提出的通用构建方法填补了适当贝叶斯极小极大多重收缩估计量的理论空白，为实践中存在多个可行收缩目标时的估计问题提供了理论保障和具体方案。

Abstract: For the canonical problem of estimating a multivariate normal mean under squared error loss, we demonstrate, for the first time, the existence of proper Bayes minimax multiple shrinkage estimators by introducing a general approach for their explicit construction. As opposed to minimax shrinkage estimators that shrink towards a single prespecified target, minimax multiple shrinkage estimators adaptively shrink towards the more promising of a set of prespecified targets, substantially increasing the region of potential risk reduction while maintaining the protection of always being at least as good as the maximum likelihood estimator. These estimators are particularly useful in practice as they address the challenge of selecting a minimax shrinkage estimator when prior information suggests more than one viable shrinkage target to choose from. In contrast to previous formal Bayes minimax multiple shrinkage estimators, which were built on mixtures of superharmonic marginals, these proper Bayes minimax multiple shrinkage estimators are obtained via mixtures of square-root superharmonic marginals. Examples of such proper Bayes minimax multiple shrinkage estimators include an adaptive convex combination of the classical Strawderman shrinkage estimators.

</details>


### [120] [Recovery of latent inner products from an anisotropic Gaussian random geometric graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23723)
*Cheng Mao,Vidya Muthukumar*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究各向异性高斯潜在点随机几何图中的潜在内积恢复问题。针对非各向同性导致的度波动，采用双重中心化邻接矩阵的秩-d谱近似估计潜在内积，其均方误差率与协方差矩阵的稳定秩相关，且允许病态协方差矩阵，估计速率达到各向同性情况的最优水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机几何图的内积恢复研究多假设潜在点为各向同性高斯分布，而实际应用中潜在点往往具有各向异性（协方差矩阵非单位阵），导致图度分布波动剧烈，传统方法失效。本文旨在提出适用于各向异性潜在点的内积估计方法。

Method: 1) 对图邻接矩阵进行双重中心化（减去行列均值）以消除各向异性引起的度波动；2) 对双重中心化矩阵进行秩-d谱近似（即取前d个奇异值/特征值对应的低秩近似）作为潜在内积的估计量；3) 利用Hermite展开分析双重中心化邻接矩阵的每个元素，并采用Kaushik等人(2025)的去耦引理控制非线性误差项。

Result: 估计量的均方误差率由协方差矩阵Σ的稳定秩决定，该速率与各向同性情况（Σ=I_d）下已知的最优速率相匹配，且允许协方差矩阵的条件数发散（即高度病态），展现了方法对严重各向异性的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的双重中心化加谱近似方法有效解决了各向异性潜在点随机几何图中的内积恢复问题，理论结果表明该方法在估计精度上达到最优，且对潜在点分布的协方差结构具有高度适应性。

Abstract: We study the problem of recovering latent inner products from a random geometric graph with anisotropic Gaussian latent points. More precisely, for an i.i.d. sample $x_1, \dots, x_n \sim N(0,Σ)$ where $Σ\in \mathbb{R}^{d \times d}$, an edge $(i,j)$ is present in the graph if and only if $\langle x_i, x_j \rangle \ge ζ$ for a threshold $ζ$. We assume the threshold $ζ$ to be chosen such that the average edge density of the graph is of constant order. To address the undesired degree fluctuations amplified by the anisotropy of the latent points, we consider the doubly centered adjacency matrix of the graph, and estimate the latent inner products using a rank-$d$ spectral approximation of the doubly centered matrix. The estimator obtains a mean squared error with a rate involving the stable rank of the covariance matrix $Σ$. Notably, the rate of estimation matches the state of the art for the isotropic case $Σ= I_d$, and permits an ill-conditioned covariance matrix with a diverging condition number. The analysis of the spectral method proceeds via the entrywise Hermite expansion of the doubly centered adjacency matrix with respect to the latent inner products. Instead of the standard trace method, it uses a decoupling argument recently introduced by Kaushik, Romberg, and Muthukumar (2025) to control nonlinear error terms.

</details>


### [121] [Expected number density of critical points of smooth Gaussian random fields in arbitrary dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23903)
*Satoshi Kuriki,Takahiko Matsubara,Satoshi Iso*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文推导了光滑各向同性高斯随机场在ℝ^d上临界点的期望数量及高度分布的显式公式，其中期望数密度公式用至多一维积分表示，不依赖于维度d。方法上提出了de Bruijn定理的一个变体，以及高斯随机变量的Weierstrass卷积公式及其逆变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了获得光滑各向同性高斯随机场临界点期望数量和高度分布的显式、低计算复杂度的解析表达式，克服高维积分计算的困难。

Method: 提出了de Bruijn定理的一个变体，以及高斯随机变量的Weierstrass卷积公式及其逆变换。

Result: 得到了临界点期望数量及高度分布的显式公式，且期望数密度公式可表示为至多一维积分，与维度d无关。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效降低了高维高斯随机场临界点统计量的计算复杂度，使得结果适用于任意维度d。

Abstract: We obtain explicit formulas for the expected number and height distribution of critical points of smooth isotropic Gaussian random fields on $\mathbb{R}^d$. The expected number density formula is expressed in terms of at most one-dimensional integrals, regardless of the dimension $d$. To obtain the formulas, we provide a variant of de Bruijn's theorem, as well as Weierstrass' convolution formula with a Gaussian random variable and its inversion.

</details>


### [122] [The Phase Transition in Online PCA Depends on $n/d\log(d)$, not $n/d$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23914)
*Apratim Dey*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究在线PCA中Oja算法的主特征向量估计，发现与经典高维统计不同，在线算法需要样本量n与维度d满足n/(d log d)趋于常数才能实现非零重叠，而经典理论中仅需n/d趋于常数。论文建立了精确的相变阈值和临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维统计理论已建立常数纵横比(n/d→常数)在理解规范估计问题极限中的重要性，但本文想探究在线/流式算法中这一规律是否仍然成立，特别是Oja算法在在线PCA中的表现是否与经典批处理PCA不同。

Method: 考虑协方差矩阵Σ=θ² v₀v₀ᵀ+I，对n个iid样本运行步长为δ/d的Oja算法。在n,d→∞且n/(d log d)→γ∈(0,∞)的渐近框架下，理论分析输出向量与真特征向量v₀的内积收敛行为，建立相变阈值。

Result: 发现了新的相变：当γγ*时，内积收敛到非零常数ρ*。临界点处相关性为随机变量。关键区别在于在线PCA需要n/(d log d)→常数而非n/d→常数才能实现非零重叠。

Conclusion: 在线/流式算法与批处理PCA存在本质区别：常数纵横比对于在线PCA实现非零重叠是不充分的，需要样本量以d log d的尺度增长。这揭示了在线算法在高维统计中的独特行为。

Abstract: High dimensional statistical theory has established the importance of constant aspect ratio, when the number of dimensions ($d$) and samples ($n$) satisfy $n,d\to\infty$ with $n/d\to γ\in(0,\infty)$, in understanding the limits of canonical estimation problems. In particular, for estimating the top eigenvector of a $d\times d$ population covariance matrix from $n$ iid samples, the BBP phase transition gives a precise threshold -- a simple functional of the aspect ratio -- such that the top sample principal component attains nonzero asymptotic correlation with the truth only when the leading population eigenvalue exceeds it. In this paper, we show that for online / streaming algorithms the story is very different, and constant aspect ratio is insufficient for nonzero overlap. We study Oja's algorithm, the most popular method for online PCA. Let $Σ=θ^2 v_0v_0^\top+I\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}$, and run Oja's algorithm with step size $δ/d$ on $n$ iid samples $X_k\sim\mathcal{N}(0,Σ)$, with output $\hat v_n$. Then, as $n,d\to\infty$ with $n/d\log d\toγ\in(0,\infty)$, we establish a phase transition: $|\langle\hat v_n,v_0\rangle|\to 0$ when $γ<γ_*$, and $\toρ_*$ when $γ>γ_*$. Here $ρ_*=ρ_*(θ,δ)=\sqrt{(θ^2-δ/2)_+/θ^2(1+δ/2)}$ and $γ_*=γ_*(θ,δ)=1/2δ(θ^2-δ/2)_+$. Further, at criticality, when $n=[γ_*d\log d+ηd]$ and $d\to\infty$, $η\in\mathbb{R}$, the correlation is random: $|\langle\hat v_n,v_0\rangle|\stackrel{w}{\to}ρ_*|G|\exp(η/2γ_*)/\sqrt{ρ_*^4+G^2\exp(η/γ_*)}$ where $G\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)$. This is in stark contrast to ordinary high dimensional PCA, where nonzero overlap is possible at constant $n/d$ and improves as $n/d$ increases.

</details>


### [123] [Estimating eigenvectors and eigenspaces of covariance matrices: Optimal Bounds and Conditions for Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23964)
*Phuc Tran,Van Vu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对高维协方差矩阵的特征向量与特征空间估计问题，给出了在温和假设下匹配阶的最优误差界，并推导出估计量一致性的充要条件——只需样本量 n 与 M 的有效秩线性相关，该有效秩可远小于维度 d。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据中，样本协方差矩阵常被用于估计总体协方差矩阵的特征向量和特征空间，但高维场景下传统估计的误差分析尚不完善，现有结果要么假设过强，要么界不紧。本文旨在提供最优（即上下界匹配）的误差分析。

Method: 理论推导。在高维框架（d→∞）下，对零均值随机向量 X 的协方差矩阵 M 施加温和假设，分析样本协方差矩阵 M̃ 的特征向量/特征空间估计误差，推导出上下界并证明其阶匹配。

Result: 得到了匹配阶的上界和下界；给出了估计量一致性的充要条件，即 n 只需与 M 的有效秩线性相关（有效秩可远小于 d）。

Conclusion: 本文在高维协方差矩阵特征分析中建立了最优误差界，并揭示了估计一致性仅依赖于有效秩而非全维度，为实际高维统计推断提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Let $X = [ ξ_1, \,\, ξ_2,...\,\, ,ξ_d]^\top$ be a zero-mean random vector of large dimension $d$ ($d \rightarrow \infty$) with (hidden) covariance matrix $M = (m_{ij})_{1 \leq i, j \leq d},$ where $m_{ij} = m_{ji} = \textbf{Cov}(ξ_i, ξ_j).$ Let $X_1, X_2, \dots, X_n$ be $n$ iid samples of $X$. Consider the sample covariance matrix $$\textstyle \tilde{M} := \frac{1}{n} \sum_{i=1}^{n} X_i X_i^\top.$$ In practice, one frequently uses the eigenvectors and eigenspaces of $\tilde M$ as estimators for those of $M$. A central task is to provide an error analysis for these estimators.
  In this paper, we provide an optimal error analysis, obtaining upper and lower bounds of matching order of magnitude, for a wide range of parameters $d$ and $n$, under mild assumptions on $M$.
  As corollaries, we obtain new necessary and sufficient conditions for the consistency of the estimators. In these conditions, we only require the number of samples $n$ to depend linearly on the effective rank of $M$, which can be much smaller than the dimension $d$.

</details>


### [124] [Sharp Minimax Rates for Smooth Two-Sample Testing under Central Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23974)
*Ilmun Kim*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文建立了中心差分隐私下Hölder光滑密度两样本检验的极小最优极限，揭示了隐私会改变经典的光滑检验边界，产生多重 regime 的相图。最优分离半径由经典非私有速率和三种隐私障碍的最大值决定。上界通过离散化样本并应用私有离散两样本检验实现；下界结合光滑扰动构造与隐私耦合不等式。当光滑度未知时，还开发了自适应多尺度私有检验，并证明自适应代价仅出现在经典非私有项中，为一个迭代对数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在两样本假设检验中引入中心差分隐私（ε-DP）约束，探究隐私保护如何影响检测分布差异的最优速率，并建立精确的极小最优边界。

Method: 上界：对样本进行离散化，对直方图应用私有离散两样本检验，调整分箱分辨率以平衡近似偏差、抽样波动和隐私噪声。下界：结合光滑扰动构造与隐私特定的耦合及传输不等式。自适应情形：开发多尺度私有检验，并证明匹配的下界。

Result: 最优分离半径是四个项的最大值：经典非私有速率 + 三种隐私障碍。根据隐私预算和光滑度与维度之比，不同障碍主导，形成清晰的相图。自适应时额外代价为迭代对数因子，且仅出现在经典非私有项中。

Conclusion: 差分隐私从根本上改变了两样本检验的最优检测边界，产生了新的率障碍。所提方法具有有限样本I类错误控制，且自适应检验达到了最优自适应速率。

Abstract: We establish sharp minimax limits for two-sample testing of Hölder-smooth densities under central differential privacy. Given two independent samples, the goal is to decide whether the underlying distributions are identical or separated in $L_1$ distance, while releasing only an $\varepsilon$-differentially private decision. We show that privacy changes the classical smooth-testing boundary through multiple regimes: the optimal separation radius is the maximum of four terms, consisting of the classical nonprivate rate and three distinct privacy-induced barriers. Which barrier is active depends on the privacy budget and the smoothness-to-dimension ratio, yielding a sharp phase diagram. Our upper bound discretizes the samples, applies a private discrete two-sample test to the resulting histograms, and chooses the bin resolution to balance approximation bias, sampling fluctuations, and privacy noise. The procedure also admits a permutation-calibrated implementation with finite-sample type~I error control. For the lower bounds, we combine smooth perturbation constructions with privacy-specific coupling and transport inequalities, showing that all four terms are unavoidable. Finally, when the smoothness is unknown, we develop a multiscale private test that attains the optimal adaptive rate and prove a matching lower bound. Adaptation costs exactly an iterated-logarithmic factor, and this cost appears only in the classical nonprivate term.

</details>


### [125] [The Zero Pattern of a Design Matrix Drives Multiple Descent in Over-parameterized Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24041)
*Kevin Han Huang,Haoyu Ye,Somak Laha,Morgane Austern*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了在协变量相关且协方差矩阵可能退化的情况下，过参数化线性回归的预测风险，发现退化和相关性可能导致多重下降现象，并利用图论方法（最大匹配和Dulmage-Mendelsohn分解）识别方差奇异点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过参数化线性回归研究大多假设协变量独立且协方差矩阵非退化，本文旨在放松这两个假设，探索协变量依赖和协方差退化对预测风险的影响。

Method: 推导了消失岭回归下预测风险的行列式等价量；利用方差剖面的新颖图表示，通过最大匹配和Dulmage-Mendelsohn分解分析关联二分图，识别方差奇异配置。

Result: 发现协方差矩阵退化和协变量依赖可导致多重下降现象，并刻画了对应峰值可能出现的位置。

Conclusion: 协变量依赖和协方差退化是多重下降的重要来源，图论方法有效识别了方差奇异点。

Abstract: Over-parameterized linear regression has been widely studied over the last decade. However, most existing works assume that the covariates are independent and that their covariance matrices are non-degenerate. In this paper, we relax both assumptions and derive deterministic equivalents for the prediction risk in a vanishing-ridge regime. We show that degeneracy of the covariance matrices and dependence can lead to multiple descent, and characterize where the corresponding peaks can occur. Our proofs use a novel graph representation of the variance profile. We show that maximum matchings and the Dulmage--Mendelsohn decomposition of the associated bipartite graph identify the configurations at which the variance becomes singular.

</details>


### [126] [Nonparametric Drift Estimation for Multidimensional Stochastic Differential Equations under Censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24088)
*Yichuan Huang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究多维随机微分方程在时变区域删失下的漂移估计问题，证明观测过程仍为连续半鞅，并构建了自适应Nadaraya-Watson漂移估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多应用中，扩散轨迹仅在时变区域内被无失真观测，区域外仅记录最近边界点和可见性指标。经典方法仅处理一维情况，高维投影会产生额外有界变差项，需要验证删失下漂移估计所需局部信息是否保留。

Method: 通过欧几里得投影到随机时变闭凸集上形式化删失机制，在投影映射满足适当正则性条件（允许删失区域非光滑）下，证明观测过程为连续半鞅并建立广义伊藤分解。基于可见时间随机积分恒等式，构建Nadaraya-Watson型漂移估计器及数据驱动带宽选择规则。

Result: 证明了删失下观测过程仍保持半鞅结构，其有界变差修正项在可见时间上无质量；建立了可见时间随机积分恒等式；构造了漂移估计器并建立了oracle不等式；推导了全观测与删失实验的各向异性极小化下界。

Conclusion: 高维删失扩散过程的局部信息得以保留，所提估计器在理论上具有最优性保证。

Abstract: In many applications, a diffusion trajectory is observed without distortion only while it remains inside a time-varying region; outside this region, only its nearest boundary point and an indicator of visibility are recorded. We formalize this setting through a censoring scheme for multidimensional stochastic differential equations, in which the latent state is observed via its Euclidean projection onto a random, time-dependent closed convex set. Classical arguments handle the one-dimensional case. In higher dimensions, however, the projection generates additional finite-variation terms, and it is not immediate that the local information required for drift estimation is preserved under censoring. We show that it is. Under a suitable regularity condition on the projection map, allowing the censoring region to be nonsmooth, the observed process is a continuous semimartingale and admits a generalized It{ô} decomposition, whose finite-variation correction term assigns no mass to visible times. Consequently, stochastic integrals over visible times coincide exactly for the observed and latent processes. Building on this identity, we construct a Nadaraya-Watson-type drift estimator with a data-driven bandwidth selection rule and establish oracle inequalities. We also derive anisotropic minimax lower bounds for both the full-observation and censored experiments.

</details>


### [127] [Möbius-Invariant Goodness-of-Fit Tests for the Spherical Cauchy Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24376)
*Diego Bolón,Davy Paindaveine*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类基于Möbius变换的球形柯西分布拟合优度检验方法，通过将观测数据变换为近似球面均匀分布后应用投影均匀性统计量，实现了精确的无分布检验，并证明了其渐近性质与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高维单位超球面上球形柯西模型的拟合优度检验问题，现有方法存在分布依赖性强、临界值难以精确计算等局限，亟需开发精确无分布且计算可行的检验方法。

Method: 利用球形柯西族在Möbius变换下的不变性，先用样本Möbius均值估计参数，再将观测变换为近似均匀分布，最后应用投影均匀性统计量；研究了Möbius均值的理论性质及其与极大似然估计的一致性。

Result: 所得检验在零假设下精确无分布，可用蒙特卡洛逼近临界值；导出了检验统计量的渐近零分布（去除一阶球谐分量后与均匀性统计量相同）；证明了固定备择下的一致性并刻画了局部功效。

Conclusion: 该方法在理论上严格、计算上可行，蒙特卡洛实验验证了有限样本准确性及检验功效，真实数据案例进一步支持了实用性。

Abstract: We introduce a class of goodness-of-fit tests for the spherical Cauchy model on the unit hypersphere. The proposed procedures exploit the invariance of the spherical Cauchy family under Möbius transformations: after estimating the parameter by the sample Möbius mean, the observations are transformed to approximate spherical uniformity, and a projection-based uniformity statistic is applied to the resulting sample. We show that, under mild conditions, the resulting tests are exactly distribution-free under the null hypothesis, so that exact critical values can be arbitrarily well approximated by Monte Carlo simulation. We study the Möbius mean as a population functional, establish its existence and uniqueness under mild conditions, and prove equivariance and asymptotic linearity of its empirical counterpart, which coincides with the spherical Cauchy maximum likelihood estimator. We derive the asymptotic null distribution of the test statistic and show that it coincides with that of the underlying uniformity statistic after removing the degree-one spherical-harmonic component, which corresponds to the tangent space of the spherical Cauchy model. We establish consistency against fixed alternatives and characterize local powers through the spherical-harmonic decomposition of contiguous alternatives. Monte Carlo experiments demonstrate the finite-sample accuracy of the asymptotic approximations and the empirical power of the proposed tests. A real data example is treated.

</details>


### [128] [Optimal estimation of high-dimensional quantum states using locally gentle measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24491)
*Cristina Butucea,Jan Johannes,Henning Stein*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究在α-温和测量约束下估计d维量子态ρ的最优极小极大速率。结果表明，Frobenius范数下的最优估计率为d³/(nα²)（一般状态）和rd²/(nα²)（秩r状态），温和性代价d/α²与希尔伯特空间维数而非参数数量成比例。论文提出了最优温和测量方案及其物理实现方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究温和测量（不坍缩量子态）条件下的量子态估计问题，并探究其与量子差分隐私的联系。

Method: 通过量子信息论不等式推导下界；提出使用辅助态和CNOT门实现最优温和测量的物理方案；证明测量结果的似然满足局部差分隐私。

Result: 一般状态最优率d³/(nα²)；秩r状态最优率rd²/(nα²)；温和性代价与希尔伯特空间维数d相关，而非参数数量rd。

Conclusion: 温和测量下的量子态估计面临与维数相关的额外代价，这与经典差分隐私不同，具有独特的量子特性。

Abstract: We study the task of estimating a $d-$dimensional quantum state $ρ$ under the constraint that the measurement is $α-$gentle. Such measurements $M$ do not collapse the state; they issue both a random variable $R^M = ω$ containing statistical information and a post-measurement state $ρ_{M \to ω}$ such that $\|ρ_{M\to ω} - ρ\|_{Tr} \leq α$. We describe gentle measurements and their connection to quantum differential privacy.
  Our results show that the optimal minimax estimation rate in Frobenius norm is of order $d^3/(n α^2)$, instead of $d^2/n$ for general measurements. Moreover, for rank $r$ states with $r\leq d$ we prove that the optimal minimax rate is $rd^2/(n α^2)$, instead of $rd/n$. Very surprisingly, the loss for gentleness $d/α^2$ scales with the ambient dimension of the Hilbert space, rather than the number of parameters $rd$, typically seen in classical differential privacy.
  We propose optimal gentle measurements and indicate how they can be physically implemented using an ancillary state and a CNOT gate to entangle it with the initial state. We notice that the resulting random variable has a likelihood that satisfies local differential privacy. Lower bounds are proven through a new quantum information-theoretic inequality applied to well chosen families of states in the manifold of (small-rank) quantum states.

</details>
