### [129] [Clustering of large deviations in heavy-tailed moving averages: the catastrophe principle in the long-memory case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22878)
*Jiaqi Wang,Gennady Samorodnitsky*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究了重尾情况下长记忆与灾难原则的相互作用，发现长记忆会导致更广泛、更长的聚类模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究分别探讨了轻尾和重尾情况下的聚类模式（共谋原则与灾难原则），以及长记忆与共谋原则的相互作用，但长记忆与灾难原则在重尾情况下的相互作用尚未被研究。

Method: 针对双无穷滑动平均过程这一随机过程类别，理论分析长记忆条件下重尾分布的大偏差事件聚类行为。

Result: 长记忆使得灾难原则发挥作用的范围更广，导致更长且性质不同的聚类模式。

Conclusion: 长记忆显著改变了重尾情况下大偏差事件的聚类特征，使聚类模式更加复杂和持久。

Abstract: Clustering of large deviations events in a stationary stochastic process depends critically on the interplay between the tail behavior of the marginal distribution and the strength of temporal dependence. In the class of doubly infinite moving average processes, when the memory is short, previous work has established a sharp contrast in the clustering patterns between light- and heavy-tailed settings, governed by \textit{conspiracy} and \textit{catastrophe} principles respectively. It has also been described how long memory interacts with the conspiracy principle to affect clustering in the light-tailed case. This paper addresses the interaction of long memory with the catastrophe principle in the heavy-tailed case. It turns out that long memory generally allows for a wider range of catastrophes to play a role and leads to longer and qualitatively different clustering patterns.

</details>


### [130] [Mirror Langevin diffusions: Convergence rates and Markov chain approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22892)
*Benjamin Capdeville,Young-Heon Kim,Soumik Pal*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究Hessian流形上的Mirror Langevin扩散（MLD），探讨如何选择势函数$u$使得MLD对非强对数凹分布$\mu$仍能指数收敛，并提出了基于吉布斯采样的马尔可夫链近似方法，证明了其在熵正则化最优传输框架下的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否通过选择适当的势函数$u$，使得Mirror Langevin扩散即使在目标分布$\mu$不满足强对数凹性时也能实现指数收敛到平衡态。

Method: 采用Lyapunov函数方法给出MLD满足Poincaré不等式或log-Sobolev不等式的充分条件；引入两步吉布斯采样器作为MLD的马尔可夫链近似，结合熵正则化最优传输和强数据处理不等式进行分析。

Result: 给出了保证MLD指数收敛的充分条件；证明了所提马尔可夫链在$\chi^2$散度意义下具有与扩散时间尺度一致的收敛速率。

Conclusion: 通过适当选择Riemannian度规（即$\nabla^2 u$），可以扩展MLD对非强对数凹分布的适用性；所提的马尔可夫链近似方法有效且收敛速率与连续时间扩散相匹配。

Abstract: Given a strongly convex function $u$, equip $R^d$ with a Riemannian metric given by the Hessian $\nabla^2 u$. This is a so-called Hessian manifold. Given a probability density $μ$ one may run a Langevin diffusion intrinsic to the manifold with stationary distribution $μ$. Such (Hessian) manifold-valued Langevin diffusions are called Mirror Langevin diffusions (MLD) which have recently become popular. One of the questions we explore is whether, given $μ$, one can choose $u$ to get an exponential convergence to equilibrium for the MLD, especially if $μ$ is not strongly log-concave. Our results are based on Lyapunov function methods and give sufficient conditions for a Poincaré or a log-Sobolev inequality to hold for the MLD. These, in turn, imply exponential convergence. We also introduce a Markov chain approximation to the MLD given by a two step Gibbs sampler with stationary distribution $μ$. This Markov chain is a variant of the Sinkhorn Markov chain introduced in arXiv:2307.16421 that is conjectured to converge to a time-inhomogeneous generalization of the MLD. Under suitable assumptions, we prove that the Markov chain has a guaranteed convergence rate in $χ^2$ that is consistent with the diffusion time scale. Our proofs are based on ideas from entropic optimal transport and strong data processing inequalities.

</details>


### [131] [Epidemic Phase Transitions in the Zero-Range Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22908)
*Rangel Baldasso,Marcelo R. Hilario,Ian Ornelas*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个在平衡零程过程中传播感染的交互粒子系统模型，分析了感染灭绝与存活的相变图景，证明了存在非平凡临界密度：当密度足够低时感染几乎必然灭绝，密度足够高时感染以正概率存活，且在极高密度下即使瞬时治愈也能存活，同时对于任何正密度，治愈率足够小时感染也能存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Kesten和Sidoravicius提出的感染传播框架至平衡零程过程，探究感染在粒子系统中的灭绝-存活相变条件，特别是粒子密度与治愈率对感染传播的影响。

Method: 采用交互粒子系统模型，在Z^d格点上构建零程过程，感染在感染者与健康者同位置时瞬间传播，感染者以速率δ独立治愈。从仅原点粒子被感染的初始配置出发，分析不同密度ρ和治愈率δ下的感染传播行为。

Result: 证明了存在非平凡临界密度：对于任意固定正治愈率，当密度ρ充分小时感染几乎必然灭绝，当ρ充分大时感染以正概率存活；在足够高的密度下，即使治愈是瞬时的，感染也能存活；对于任意正密度，当治愈率足够小时感染也能存活。

Conclusion: 该模型展现了感染传播中密度与治愈率之间的关键权衡，确立了非平凡相变图景，为理解粒子系统中感染传播的临界条件提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We consider a model for the spread of an infection within an interacting particle system on $\mathbb{Z}^d$, generalizing a framework introduced by Kesten and Sidoravicius to a zero-range process in equilibrium with density $ρ> 0$. In our model, at any time, individuals are either healthy or infected. The infection spreads instantaneously whenever infected and healthy particles occupy the same site, while infected particles heal independently at rate $δ> 0$. We investigate the extinction-survival phase diagram of this process starting from a configuration where only the particles at the origin are infected. For every fixed positive healing rate, we prove that the infection becomes extinct almost surely if the density $ρ$ is sufficiently small and survives with positive probability if $ρ$ is sufficiently large, establishing the existence of a non-trivial critical density. At sufficiently high densities, survival occurs even under instantaneous healing. We also show that, for every positive density, the infection survives when the healing rate is sufficiently small.

</details>


### [132] [Mixing times and spectra of non-equilibrium symmetric exclusion processes on general graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22991)
*Leonard J. Schulman,Alistair Sinclair*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究了非平衡态下的对称排斥过程（SEP），针对任意图结构和任意热浴，推导了混合时间的紧界、稳态联合占据分布的计算算法，并证明了链的谱与热浴温度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡态SEP的稳态分布难以刻画，现有研究几乎仅限于有限格点图，对混合时间等关键量知之甚少，亟需更一般的理论结果。

Method: 通过将混合时间上界归结为单粒子到任意热浴的最坏 hitting time，设计运行时间约为 n^{O(k)} 的算法计算任意k个顶点的稳态联合占据分布，并分析非可逆马尔可夫链的谱性质。

Result: 得到了混合时间的紧界（至多差两个对数因子）；给出了计算k点联合分布的算法；证明了链的谱与热浴温度无关且始终为实数。

Conclusion: 该工作为非平衡态SEP在一般图上的研究提供了定量工具，特别是混合时间紧界和谱独立性结果具有理论意义，算法有望用于分析小k下的k阶相关性。

Abstract: The symmetric exclusion process (SEP) is a classical model of interacting particles on a graph, in which multiple particles execute random walks subject to the constraint that no two particles may occupy the same vertex. The version of the process in which the number of particles is fixed, is by now very well understood, through the study of reversible Markov chains.
  In this paper, we study the non-equilibrium version of the SEP, in which some vertices of the graph interact with external heat baths, held at different temperatures, that mediate transport through the graph. The resulting steady-state distribution is still the stationary distribution of a natural Markov chain, but the chain is no longer reversible. This means that even the steady-state distribution is very hard to describe, and indeed is the subject of a rich literature, which has focused almost exclusively on graphs which are finite subsets of the lattice $Z^d$. Very little is known about other important quantities, notably the mixing time.
  We derive various quantitative results about the non-equilibrium SEP for arbitrary graphs with arbitrary heat baths. Our main result is a bound on the mixing time in terms of the worst-case hitting time of a single particle to any of the heat baths; this bound is tight up to two logarithmic factors in the size of the graph. We then use this result to derive a simple algorithm, running in time roughly $n^{O(k)}$, that computes the steady-state joint occupation distribution for any set of $k$ vertices; since the steady-state distribution is very elusive, this provides a potentially useful tool to study its $k$-wise correlations for small values of $k$. Finally, we prove that the spectrum of the Markov chain associated with the non-reversible dynamics for the SEP is independent of the heat bath temperatures; thus in particular the spectrum is always real.

</details>


### [133] [Level-set entropy and sparse randomized embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23017)
*Konstantin Tikhomirov*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏随机矩阵与固定子空间等距算子的乘积谱范数，在特定条件下证明了高概率上界为C√(kp)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机维度缩减中，乘积ΠU_V是核心模型，已有研究主要通过迹不等式和高斯比较不等式，本文旨在发展基于熵估计的新方法。

Method: 基于向量x∈V的熵估计方法，结合已有估计，分析稀疏随机矩阵（伯努利-乘积型条目）与子空间等距算子的乘积谱范数。

Result: 当k≥C r (log log r)²且p≥(log k)/k时，以高概率有‖ΠU_V‖≤C√(kp)。对其他负相关条目模型也有匹配结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的熵估计方法有效建立了稀疏随机矩阵乘积的谱范数上界，拓展了随机维度缩减的理论工具。

Abstract: Let $Π$ be a $k\times n$ sparse random matrix. For a fixed $r$-dimensional subspace $V\subset{\mathbb R}^n$, let $U_V:{\mathbb R}^r\to{\mathbb R}^n$ denote an isometry from ${\mathbb R}^r$ onto $V$. The product $ΠU_V$ is a central model in randomized dimension reduction and has been studied primarily through trace and Gaussian comparison inequalities. In this work, we develop an approach to the spectral norm of the matrix product $ΠU_V$, based on entropy estimates for level sets of vectors $x\in V$. Combining the method with existing estimates, we show the following. Assume that \[
  k\ge C\,r(\log\log r)^2,\qquad
  p\ge (\log k)/k. \] Let $Π$ be a $k\times n$ matrix with i.i.d. entries equidistributed with the product $b\,ξ$, where $b$ is a Bernoulli($p$) random variable and $ξ$ is mean-zero, independent of $b$, and satisfies $|ξ|\le1$ almost surely. Then with high probability \[
  \|ΠU_V\|\le C\sqrt{kp}. \] Matching results hold for other random models with negatively associated entries.

</details>


### [134] [A Variance Representation Formula for Hölder's Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23255)
*Li-Chang Hung*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文建立了Hölder不等式亏量的精确表示公式，将其刻画为沿自然指数插值路径累积的方差，揭示了不等式背后的凸性本质与插值能量解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅通过辅助量对Hölder不等式的亏量进行估计，缺乏精确的解析表示；本文旨在建立亏量的精确表达式，揭示其内在结构。

Method: 利用对数密度比的方差，结合一维插值参数的Green核，将对数Hölder亏量表示为沿指数插值路径的积分形式。

Result: 获得了Hölder不等式亏量的精确表示公式，该公式等价于对数分配函数的凸性，并将亏量诠释为插值能量。

Conclusion: 该表示方法为通过方差恒等式研究更广泛的泛函不等式提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We establish an exact representation formula for the deficit in Hölder's inequality. Rather than estimating the deficit by auxiliary quantities, we show that it can be represented as an accumulated variance along the natural exponential interpolation connecting the two endpoint densities.
  More precisely, the logarithmic Hölder deficit is expressed as the integral of the variance of the logarithmic density ratio weighted by the Green kernel associated with the one-dimensional interpolation parameter.
  This identity reveals that Hölder's inequality is a consequence of the convexity of a logarithmic partition function and provides an intrinsic interpretation of the deficit as an interpolation energy.
  The representation suggests a broader framework for studying functional inequalities through variance identities.

</details>


### [135] [Entropic analogues of Grünbaum's inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23269)
*Matthieu Fradelizi,Lampros Gavalakis,Martin Rapaport*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文证明了对数凹随机变量的熵不等式，给出了微分熵和Rényi熵在条件分布下的上下界，刻画了等号成立条件，并讨论了高维推广的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Grünbaum不等式及其函数推广的启发，作者希望建立熵版本的类似不等式：即对数凹随机变量在给定均值条件下的条件熵与原始熵之间的定量关系，并刻画等号成立情形。

Method: 利用自由度（degrees of freedom）技术和KKT型优化引理，结合二进制熵函数的性质，证明了新的不等式；同时通过构造反例讨论高维推广的局限性。

Result: 得到微分熵的上界和下界：h(X|X≤EX)介于h(X)-e/(e-1)H₂(1/e)和h(X)之间；对于Rényi熵和最小熵也得到了类似结果；刻画了所有等号成立情形。

Conclusion: 论文建立了对数凹随机变量熵版本的Grünbaum不等式，证明是尖锐的并完整刻画了等号情形，同时指出了高维推广的困难与反例。

Abstract: The classical Grünbaum inequality asserts that the proportion of the volume of a convex body cut off by a halfspace containing its barycenter is at least $1/e$. From its functional counterpart, for any log-concave random variable $X$, one has $\mathbb{P}(X\ge \mathbb{E}X)\ge 1/e$, with equality if and only if $X$ is exponential. Motivated by Grünbaum's inequality for convex bodies and its functional generalizations, we prove analogous inequalities for entropy, with characterizations of the equality cases. We show that if $X$ is a log-concave random variable on $\mathbb{R}$, then $$
  h(X)-\frac{e}{e-1}H_2(1/e) \leq h(X|X \leq \mathbb{E}X) \leq h(X), $$ where $h$ is the differential entropy, $H_2(\cdot)$ is the binary entropy function and $X|X\leq \mathbb{E}X$ stands for the distribution of $X$ conditional on $X\leq \mathbb{E}X$. We generalize the upper bound for all Rényi entropies and the lower bound for min-entropy. Our inequalities are sharp and we characterize all equality cases. We discuss potential generalizations in high dimensions and give counterexamples in some directions. As an intermediate step for the proof of the lower bound, we establish a new inequality that we prove using a technique known as degrees of freedom, combined with a standard KKT-type optimization lemma. Along the way, we characterize the equality case in a known comparison inequality between differential and min-entropy, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [136] [On extremes of a random walk with positive drift over an intermediate regularly varying time interval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23305)
*Sergey Foss,Dmitry Korshunov*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 研究有限正漂移随机游走在中间正则变化停时下的尾部分布，发现停时尾部主导了S_τ和M_τ的尾部渐近行为，随机游走仅通过大数定律贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究停时分布尾部重于跳跃分布时，随机游走和停时对复合随机游走尾部行为的贡献机制。

Method: 基于中间正则变化分布与轻尾跳跃假设，利用大数定律和渐近分析方法推导尾部等价性。

Result: S_τ和M_τ的尾部分布渐近等价，且完全由停时τ的尾部决定，随机游走仅通过其漂移（大数定律）贡献。

Conclusion: 在给定条件下，复合随机游走的尾部行为由停时的尾部主导，跳跃分布的细节不产生渐近影响。

Abstract: We consider a random walk $\{S_n\}$ with a finite positive drift that is stopped at a random time $τ$ having an intermediate regularly varying distribution. We assume that the jump distribution is lighter-tailed than the distribution of $τ$. Under these conditions, we show that the tails of the distributions of $S_τ$ and $M_τ = \max_{k\le τ} S_k$ are asymptotically equivalent and are determined by the tail of $τ$, while the random walk $\{S_n\}$ contributes only through the law of large numbers.

</details>


### [137] [Non-Gaussianity of the Stagnation Law in Particle Swarm Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23381)
*Alexandra - Ionela Andriciuc,David - Corneliu Turturean,Ionel Popescu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文证明了在粒子群优化算法停滞状态下，当满足均方稳定条件时，位置边际分布不可能是高斯分布，解决了粒子群优化领域的一个公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决粒子群优化算法中一个长期存在的公开问题（Problem 18），即确定在特定条件下位置边际分布是否可能为高斯分布。

Method: 通过比较平稳矩方程与高斯矩恒等式至八阶，利用Hermite多项式给出四阶和六阶相容性条件，再通过八阶方程排除所有可能解；使用模23和模1,000,003的算术验证，以及原始矩实现产生精确多项式Q4, Q6, Q8进行验证。

Result: 证明了在整个均方稳定区域内，不存在高斯不变位置边际分布，即位置边际分布不可能是高斯的。四阶和六阶条件存在交点，但八阶条件将其排除，表明低阶高斯诊断是不充分的。

Conclusion: 在粒子群优化停滞状态下，位置边际分布不可能是高斯分布，这一结论通过严格的数学证明和多种验证方式得到确认。

Abstract: We study one-dimensional particle swarm optimization during stagnation, with two fixed distinct attractors and equal independent uniform acceleration ranges. The position then satisfies a second-order random affine recurrence. For inertia $w$ and acceleration range $c$, we prove that throughout the open mean-square stability region \[
  -1<w<1,\qquad c>0,\qquad 12(1-w^2)-c(7-5w)>0, \] no invariant position marginal, and hence no limiting position marginal, can be Gaussian. This solves the open Problem 18 in \cite{ParticleSwarmProblems}. The proof compares the stationary moment equations with the Gaussian moment identities through order eight. A Hermite-polynomial formulation gives explicit fourth- and sixth-order compatibility conditions whose common solutions lie on a degree-$107$ polynomial branch. Exact eighth-order equations exclude every point on that branch. The final certificate is verified using arithmetic modulo $23$ and independently modulo $1{,}000{,}003$. A separate raw-moment implementation produces exact polynomials $Q_4,Q_6,Q_8$ in $(w,c)$ and verifies the same obstruction over the rational numbers. The fourth- and sixth-order curves have a genuine admissible intersection, but the eighth-order condition removes it, showing why low-order Gaussian diagnostics are insufficient. All code, exact polynomials, logs, and plot-validation data are supplied as online resources.

</details>


### [138] [Limit Theorems for the Pitman-Yor Frequency Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23401)
*Ross Arthur Maller,Soudabeh Shemehsavar*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文推导了适用于Gibbs型划分的通用分布公式，并用于分析Pitman-Yor抽样模型中等位基因频率谱的线性组合的渐近分布，提出了函数极限定理的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了获得Gibbs型划分中分量频率谱线性组合的大样本结果，特别是遗传学中的等位基因频率谱。

Method: 推导适用于Gibbs型划分的通用分布公式，详细分析两参数Pitman-Yor抽样模型，获得特定和式的渐近分布。

Result: 获得了形如∑_{j=⌊λn⌋}^{⌊μn⌋} M_{jn}（0<λ≤μ≤1）的渐近分布，并提示了∑_{j=⌊λn⌋}^{n} M_{jn}可能的函数极限定理。

Conclusion: 研究结果与划分集上随机结构的极限形状存在有用联系，并建议了其他应用方向。

Abstract: We derive a general distribution formula applicable to a wide variety of Gibbs-type partitions and use it to obtain large sample results for linear combinations of the component frequency spectrum $(M_{jn})_{1\le j\le n}$ (in genetics, the allele frequency spectrum) associated with a random partitioning of $\{1,2,\ldots, n\}$. The two-parameter Pitman-Yor sampling model is analysed in detail and asymptotic distributions of sums of the form $\sum _{j=\lf λn\rf}^{\lf μn\rf} M_{jn}$, $0<λ\le μ\le 1$, are obtained. Our results suggest a possible functional limit theorem for $\sum _{j=\lf λn\rf}^{n} M_{jn}$. Useful connections with limit shapes for random structures on the set of partitions and other applications are suggested.

</details>


### [139] [A note on Lata\la's argument in SK model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23427)
*Seiichiro Kusuoka,Shuta Nakajima*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文通过改进Latała的论证并使用Kearns-Saul不等式，证明了在特定条件下Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型的自由能收敛到副本对称公式，误差为O(N^{-1})，并将适用范围从β<1/2扩展到包括β>1的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型中自由能收敛到副本对称公式的适用范围，超越此前β<1/2的限制。

Method: 通过改进Latała的论证方法，结合Kearns-Saul不等式，推导出更宽松的收敛条件。

Result: 证明了在条件β²·q/arctanh(q) < 1下，重叠浓度和自由能收敛到副本对称公式，误差为O(N^{-1})。该条件在β<1时始终成立，且当h≠0时包含β>1的非空区间。

Conclusion: 成功将SK模型自由能收敛到副本对称公式的证明从β<1/2扩展到更广的参数区域，包括部分β>1的情况。

Abstract: In this note, we consider the Sherrington--Kirkpatrick model with deterministic external field. Let $q=q(β,h)$ denote the solution of the replica-symmetric self-consistency equation \[ q=\mathbb E\tanh^2\!\left(h+β\sqrt q\,Z\right), \qquad Z\sim N(0,1), \] where $β$ and $h$ are inverse temperature and external field, respectively.
  By refining Lata\la' s argument, previously limited to \(β< \frac{1}{2}\), and using the Kearns--Saul inequality, we prove overlap concentration and convergence of the free energy to the replica symmetric formula with error \(O(N^{-1})\) whenever
  \[ β^2\frac{q}{{\rm arctanh}q}<1. \]
  Note that for any $β<1$ and $h\in \mathbb R$, the condition above is satisfied. Moreover, for every nonzero $h$, this region contains a nonempty interval with $β>1$.

</details>


### [140] [Equivalence of Canonical and Microcanonical Ensembles for Euclidean Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23526)
*Ziyang Zhu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文在欧几里得格点上建立了正则系综与微正则系综在局部能量尺度下的等价性，并得到了精确计数渐近与边际分布全变差收敛，同时指出了非格点情形下的失效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究欧几里得格点上多副本非相互作用系统的统计力学系综等价性问题，特别是在局部能量尺度下正则系综与微正则系综的关系。

Method: 通过格点值能量的可达精确壳层与固定宽度窗口两种情形，利用Bost稳定Arakelov格点计数理论得到的熵函数，证明指数速率与计数渐近。

Result: 在格点情形下，可达精确壳层与固定宽度窗口均实现了系综等价，得到精确计数渐近与全变差收敛；非格点情形下精确壳层等价失效。

Conclusion: 正则与微正则系综在局部能量尺度下对于格点系统等价，且指数速率恢复Bost的Arakelov格点计数熵函数；非格点情形则存在本质差异。

Abstract: Let $Λ$ be a Euclidean lattice, and consider $N$ labelled, noninteracting copies with one-site energy $H=\lVert\cdot\rVert^2$. We prove equivalence of canonical Gibbs and microcanonical ensembles at a local energy scale: on reachable exact shells for lattice-valued energies and in fixed-width windows otherwise. We obtain sharp counting asymptotics and total-variation convergence of every fixed marginal, but show that exact-shell equivalence can fail in the nonlattice case. The exponential rates in both regimes recover the entropy function arising from Bost's stable Arakelov lattice-point counting.

</details>


### [141] [A CIR-Type Diffusion Driven by Hermite Processes: Well-Posedness, Positivity and Malliavin Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23548)
*Atef Lechiheb*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类广义Cox-Ingersoll-Ross扩散模型，其中驱动过程为Hermite过程（含长记忆与非高斯特性），并在Young-Stieltjes意义下建立了路径化解的存在唯一性、正性概率界、Malliavin可微性及绝对连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典CIR模型广泛应用于金融与生物学，但仅能处理布朗运动驱动的短记忆、高斯情形。实际数据常呈现长程依赖与非高斯特征，亟需在更一般的随机驱动下建立CIR型扩散的严格理论。

Method: 利用Young-Stieltjes积分处理非半鞅的Hermite过程（阶数q≥1，Hurst参数H∈(1/2,1)）；采用光滑化平方根函数φ_ε保证Lipschitz系数；借助分数阶Sobolev空间、Malliavin微积分及Girsanov型变换建立分析框架。

Result: (1) Young-Stieltjes意义下强解的存在唯一性；(2) 当σ₁=0时，过程在[0,T]上保持正性的概率下界显式表达式；(3) X_t属于Malliavin空间D^{1,∞}，且导数为线性化Young SDE的解；(4) X_t的分布在t>0时关于Lebesgue测度绝对连续。

Conclusion: 成功将CIR模型推广至Hermite过程驱动场景，兼顾长记忆、非高斯性与正性保持，并建立了正则性结果。但正性的几乎必然结论在非半鞅情形下仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We study a generalized Cox--Ingersoll--Ross (CIR) diffusion \begin{equation*}
  dX_t = a\bigl(b(t)-X_t\bigr)\,dt
  + \bigl(σ_0+σ_1\sqrt{φ_\eps(X_t)}\bigr)\,dZ_t^{(q,H)},
  \quad X_0 = x_0 > 0, \end{equation*} where $Z^{(q,H)}$ is a Hermite process of order $q\geq 1$ and Hurst parameter $H\in(1/2,1)$, and $φ_\eps$ is a smooth regularisation of the square root. This framework simultaneously captures long-range dependence, non-Gaussian innovations (for $q\geq 2$), and the positivity of the classical CIR model. Since Hermite processes with $q\geq 2$ are not semimartingales, we interpret the dynamics pathwise in the Young--Stieltjes sense, exploiting the Hölder regularity of $Z^{(q,H)}$.
  We establish four main results. First, well-posedness: a unique strong solution exists in a fractional Sobolev space, under globally Lipschitz coefficients (satisfied by $φ_\eps$). Second, a quantitative positivity bound: for $σ_1=0$, the probability that $X$ stays positive on $[0,T]$ is bounded below by an explicit expression tending to $1$ as the initial level and long-run target grow large relative to $σ_0$; an almost-sure statement, available in the classical Brownian case, is not established here, since the usual boundary-non-attainment mechanism relies on tools unavailable for a non-semimartingale driver. Third, Malliavin differentiability: $X_t\in\D^{1,\infty}$, with an explicit formula for the Malliavin derivative as the solution of a linearised Young SDE. Fourth, absolute continuity: the law of $X_t$ is absolutely continuous with respect to the Lebesgue measure for all $t>0$.

</details>


### [142] [The One-Period Kyle (1985) Model Has a Unique Equilibrium: A Monotone Gaussian Bayes inverse-rigidity theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23585)
*Rabee Tourky*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文证明了在Kyle（1985）内幕交易模型中，若交易策略满足某个最大化条件，则该策略必为恒等函数，从而证明了该模型中唯一均衡是Kyle的线性策略，且竞争性定价规则必然为线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决金融经济学中长期悬而未决的问题：证明Kyle内幕交易模型中均衡的唯一性，特别是定价规则线性化这一关键性质。

Method: 采用实变量与概率论方法，不依赖任何正则性假设（仅需Borel可测性），通过高斯随机单调函数的上下界逼迫使相等情况只允许恒等函数。

Result: 证明了若对任意v，φ(v)最大化x(v - F_φ(x))，则φ必为恒等函数；从而Kyle仿射策略是唯一均衡，竞争性定价规则必然线性。

Conclusion: 该结果彻底解决了Kyle模型中均衡唯一性的长期悬疑问题，且证明方法不依赖解析性等强假设，更为简洁和基础。

Abstract: Let $V$ and $U$ be independent standard normal random variables. For any Borel map $φ\colon\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{R}$, set $Y_φ=φ(V)+U$, and define $P_φ(y)=\mathbb{E}[V\mid Y_φ=y]$ and $F_φ(x)=\mathbb{E}[P_φ(x+U)]$. We prove that, if for every $v\in\mathbb{R}$, the quantity $φ(v)$ maximises $x(v-F_φ(x))$ over $x\in\mathbb{R}$, then $φ$ is the identity function.
  This is the normalised one-period Kyle (1985) model of insider trading. It follows that Kyle's closed-form affine strategy is the unique equilibrium of the model for arbitrary Gaussian location and scale, and that its canonical competitive pricing rule is necessarily linear. This settles a long-standing question in financial economics.
  Boulatov, Kyle and Livdan (2005, 2013) introduced complex-analytic techniques to the problem. McLennan, Monteiro and Tourky (2017) proved a linear growth bound for $P_φ$, real-entire regularity of $F_φ$, and uniqueness when an equilibrium strategy agrees locally with a uniquely continuable analytic function. The present proof requires no regularity assumption on $φ$ beyond Borel measurability. Its argument is real-variable and probabilistic: the maximisation forces sharp upper and lower bounds for Gaussian-randomised monotone functions to coincide, and the corresponding equality cases admit only the identity function.

</details>


### [143] [Non-equilibrium fluctuations of a two-species exclusion process with slow boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23640)
*Ziying Chen,Yong Li*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 研究了一维双物种对称简单排他过程在临界慢边界储层下的非平衡密度涨落，证明了耦合涨落场收敛到广义Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解双物种系统在慢边界条件下的非平衡密度涨落行为，揭示体扩散、物种转换和边界储层的综合效应。

Method: 建立双物种对称简单排他过程的数学模型，设置边界率为1/n量级的临界慢边界，通过耦合涨落场分析，利用线性鞅问题方法证明收敛性。

Result: 耦合涨落场收敛到广义Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程，该过程的系数反映了体扩散、物种转换和边界储层的综合作用。

Conclusion: 成功描述了双物种系统在临界慢边界下的非平衡涨落行为，为理解复杂边界条件下的多物种输运现象提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study the non-equilibrium density fluctuations of a one-dimensional two-species symmetric simple exclusion process in contact with critical slow boundary reservoirs. The boundary rates are of order $1/n$, which leads to Robin boundary conditions at the macroscopic level. We prove that the coupled fluctuation field associated with the empirical densities of two species converges to a generalized Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process. The limiting process is characterized by a linear martingale problem whose coefficients reflect the combined effects of bulk diffusion, species conversion, and the boundary reservoirs.

</details>


### [144] [Precise cover times for branching random walks on Hamming graphs: (iterated) logarithmic corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23791)
*Zhenyuan Zhang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hamming图上分支随机游走的覆盖时间的渐近精确表达式，发现了b>2与b=2两种情况下的不同渐近行为，主要差异源于最后未覆盖区域的几何结构不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 之前的研究仅给出了覆盖时间的线性阶估计，本文旨在得到更精确的渐近表达式，揭示不同参数下的精细行为差异。

Method: 结合经典的脊柱测度变换技巧、多对少估计、家系的多尺度分解以及早期种群的加权鞅分析。

Result: 对于b>2，覆盖时间τ_cov(d)=x_* d+λ^{-1}log d+O_P(1)；对于b=2，τ_cov(d)=x_* d+χ^{-1}log log d+O_P(1)，其中x_*和χ为显式正常数。

Conclusion: 覆盖时间的渐近行为存在二分法：b>2时有指数多个对跖点，而二元超立方体有唯一对跖点及其邻域决定最终覆盖。

Abstract: We prove tight asymptotics of the cover time $τ_{\mathrm{cov}}(d)$ of a continuous-time branching random walk on the Hamming graph $\{0,1,\dots,b-1\}^d$, as $d\to\infty$. We focus on the slow-branching regime, where particles move at rate one and branch at rate $λ\in(0,1)$. For $b>2$, we show that $τ_{\mathrm{cov}}(d)=x_\star d+λ^{-1}\log d+O_{\mathbb P}(1)$. For $b=2$, we show that $τ_{\mathrm{cov}}(d)=x_\star d+χ^{-1}\log\log d+O_{\mathbb P}(1)$. Here, $x_\star$ and $χ$ are explicit positive constants depending only on $b$ and $λ$. Our results sharpen previously known linear-order estimates. The dichotomy reflects the geometry of the last uncovered region: for $b>2$, there are exponentially many antipodes, whereas the binary hypercube has a unique antipode and its neighbors govern the final coverage. Our proofs combine classic spine change of measure techniques and many-to-few estimates with a multiscale decomposition of the genealogy and a weighted martingale analysis of the early population.

</details>


### [145] [A face-isolation proof of the two-variable Gaussian Moments Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23887)
*Michael Wilson*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文证明了对独立标准实高斯随机变量(X,Y)，若复系数多项式P满足所有正幂次期望为零，则其单项式支撑在权重wt(Z^aW^b)=a-b下严格单侧。由此导出二维高斯矩猜想以显式阈值m>deg Q成立，并通过牛顿多边形暴露面的素隔离定理、p-adic赋值论证及Frobenius约简完成证明，最终确定了高斯矩猜想成立的维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯矩猜想探讨多项式在高斯随机变量下的矩消没条件，是概率与代数几何交叉领域的重要问题。作者欲确定该猜想成立的维度范围，填补二维情形证明的空白，并揭示其与一维情形的联系及高维反例的边界。

Method: 引入复坐标Z,W定义权重，通过牛顿多边形暴露面的素隔离定理，利用p-adic赋值分离选定面的贡献；运用Frobenius约简迫使单变量Laurent多项式正幂次常数项消没，结合Duistermaat-van der Kallen定理与平面凸几何论证排除双号支撑。

Result: 证明二维高斯矩猜想成立，显式阈值为m>deg Q；确定该猜想仅在维度n=1,2成立，n≥3存在反例。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了高斯矩猜想成立的维度条件，其核心贡献在于将复分析、p-adic赋值与凸几何方法结合，为概率矩问题提供了强有力的代数几何工具。

Abstract: Let $(X,Y)$ be independent standard real Gaussian random variables, and let $(P\in\mathbb C[X,Y])$ satisfy $\mathbb E!\left(P(X,Y)^m\right)=0$ for every $(m\ge1)$. Using the complex coordinates $Z=\frac{X+iY}{\sqrt2}, \qquad W=\frac{X-iY}{\sqrt2},$ we prove that the monomial support of $(P)$ is strictly one-sided with respect to the weight $\operatorname{wt}(Z^aW^b)=a-b$. Thus either every monomial occurring in $(P)$ has positive weight, or every monomial has negative weight. It follows that, for every $(Q\in\mathbb C[X,Y])$, $\mathbb E!\left(Q(X,Y)P(X,Y)^m\right)=0$ whenever $(m>°Q)$. This proves the two-variable Gaussian Moments Conjecture with the explicit threshold $m\ge°Q+1$.
  The main ingredient is a prime-isolation theorem for exposed faces of the Newton polygon of $(P)$. A $(p)$-adic valuation argument at moment indices of the form $(m=qp)$ separates the contribution of a chosen face from all remaining multinomial strata. Frobenius reduction then forces the constant terms of all positive powers of the associated one-variable Laurent polynomial to vanish. The theorem of Duistermaat and van der Kallen implies that the face has weights of one strict sign, while a planar convex-geometric argument rules out support containing weights of both signs. Combined with the known one-variable case and counterexamples in dimensions $(n\ge3)$, this determines the dimensions in which the Gaussian Moments Conjecture holds.

</details>


### [146] [Sharp small-deviation inequalities for sums of independent nonnegative random variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23980)
*Weibo Fu,Yanjun Han,Guanyang Wang,Jun Yan,Peng Zhang,Zhengqing Zhou*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文证明了对独立非负随机变量和的下界不等式，并肯定解决了Feige猜想（当δ≥1时），该证明结合了Dirichlet校准定理、凸几何中的Grünbaum质心定理及其推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明Feige猜想（2004）在δ≥1条件下成立，解决该领域长期悬而未决的概率下界问题。

Method: 结合Vlassis和Thomas的精确Dirichlet校准定理（解决了Gaffke猜想）、Grünbaum质心定理（1960）及其Letwin和Yaskin（2024）的推广，由ChatGPT 5.6 Pro完成证明。

Result: 对任意独立非负随机变量Xi（E[Xi]≤1），S=∑Xi，证明了P(S < E[S]+δ) ≥ b_{n,δ}，其中b_{n,δ}的表达式在δ<1和δ≥1两种情形给出，且对δ≥1该界是紧的。

Conclusion: 肯定证明了Feige猜想在δ≥1时成立，且下界至少为e^{-1}。

Abstract: Let $(X_1,\ldots,X_n)$ be independent nonnegative random variables with $\mathbb{E} X_i\le1$, and write $S=\sum_iX_i$. For $δ>0$, we prove that \[
  \mathbb{P}\left(S<\mathbb{E} S+δ\right)\ge b_{n,δ}, \] where $b_{n,δ}=δ(n/(n+δ))^n$ for $0<δ<1$ and $b_{n,δ}=(1-1/(n+δ))^n$ for $δ\ge1$. The bound is sharp for every $n$ and $δ\ge 1$. In particular, since $b_{n,δ} \ge e^{-1}$ for $δ\ge 1$, our result proves Feige's conjecture [Feige, 2004] in the affirmative for $δ\ge 1$.
  The proof is found by ChatGPT 5.6 Pro. It combines the exact Dirichlet calibration theorem of Vlassis and Thomas [Vlassis and Thomas, 2026], which resolves Gaffke's conjecture in statistics, with results in convex geometry including Grünbaum's centroid theorem [Grünbaum, 1960] and its generalization by Letwin and Yaskin [Letwin and Yaskin, 2024].

</details>


### [147] [Jeu de taquin forests and the inverse infinite RSK correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24018)
*Dan Romik,Piotr Śniady*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了由均匀分布随机序列通过无限RSK对应产生的Plancherel随机无限杨图，利用jeu de taquin森林结构给出了逆映射的几何刻画，证明了树几乎必然以确定方向延伸至无穷，建立了树与输入序列值之间的典范双射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为无限RSK对应的逆映射提供显式的几何刻画，利用先前建立的动力系统同构，探索未被充分研究的jeu de taquin森林结构。

Method: 在无限杨图上构建jeu de taquin森林，通过局部比较规则连接盒子；分析树的渐近方向性，利用迭代jeu de taquin变换下的树生命周期恢复序列顺序。

Result: 证明了每一棵树几乎必然具有定义良好的渐近方向延伸至无穷；建立了树与i.i.d.输入序列值之间的典范一一对应关系。

Conclusion: 通过jeu de taquin森林结构成功给出了逆映射的几何解释，为无限杨图与随机序列之间的对应提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The Plancherel-random infinite Young tableau arises from applying the infinite Robinson-Schensted-Knuth (RSK) correspondence to a sequence of i.i.d. random variables distributed uniformly on the unit interval [0,1]. Building on the isomorphism of dynamical systems established in prior work, we provide an explicit geometric characterization of the inverse map. Our approach makes use of a previously unexplored structure, the jeu de taquin forest on the infinite tableau, where edges connect boxes according to local comparison rules. We prove that each tree in this forest almost surely extends toward infinity with a well-defined asymptotic direction, establishing a canonical bijection between trees and values in the i.i.d. input sequence. The ordering is recovered through tree lifetimes under iterated jeu de taquin transformations.

</details>


### [148] [Derangetropy Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24705)
*Masoud Ataei,Sepideh Forouzi*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"derangetropy算子"的概率密度变换工具，它仅通过秩（rank）对累积分布函数进行重加权，并系统证明了该算子的数学性质：包括在单调变化下的等变性、可精确求解的动力学、与扩散平衡时的正弦-戈登方程、变分原理选取的规范核、与Sturm-Liouville系统的幺正提升、以及Virasoro代数对偶下的共形场论解释。最终推广到多维情形，揭示了依赖关系的层状结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在构建一种新的概率密度变换算子，使其在单调变化下保持等变性且动力学可精确求解，从而统一理解概率分布的演化、凝聚、量子化与共形场论等多方面现象。

Method: 定义derangetropy算子（通过累积分布函数的固定轮廓重加权概率密度）；证明其在绝对连续分布与单调变换下的等变性；利用区间映射分析迭代动力学（Koenigs极限律）；导出连续流闭式解（Cauchy族作为不变双曲流形）；引入扩散平衡得到过阻尼正弦-戈登方程变分原理选取Dirichlet拉普拉斯基态平方作为规范核；构造幺正提升得到等谱Sturm-Liouville系统与量子地毯；提升到Virasoro代数对偶（中心荷1），将分布与disconjugate Hill势、尾部与共形权重对应；引入随机相位得到精确采样器与乘法混沌；推广到多维情形。

Result: 1) 算子迭代将任意分布凝聚到中位数，具有普适Koenigs极限律；2) 与扩散平衡时，双曲正割分布是唯一稳态全局吸引子；3) 更新为精确1比特贝叶斯后验；4) 临界Sobolev质量的强大数律被Wiener跳跃统计量控制；5) Schrödinger密度对任意有界变差数据的分形性得到证明；6) Cauchy族对应Virasoro代数的例外轨道；7) 弱缓域中对数相关的乘法混沌；8) 多维情形中依赖关系分层为守恒相互作用、最大相关驱动的扭转与平坦Sinkhorn传输。

Conclusion: Derangetropy算子是一个具有丰富数学结构的概率密度变换框架，连接了概率论、动力系统、偏微分方程、量子力学及共形场论，为分布演化的精确求解和统一理解提供了新工具。

Abstract: A derangetropy operator reweighs a probability density by a fixed profile of its own cumulative distribution function, acting through ranks alone. We prove that these operators are precisely the transformations of absolutely continuous laws equivariant under monotone changes of variable, and that they compose through interval maps, making their dynamics exactly solvable: iteration condenses every law onto its median with a universal Koenigs limit law, the continuous flow is solvable in closed form with the Cauchy family as invariant hyperbolic manifold, and balanced against diffusion the distribution function obeys an overdamped sine-Gordon equation whose unique steady law, the hyperbolic secant, is a globally stable kink. A variational principle selects the canonical kernel, the squared ground state of the Dirichlet Laplacian on the unit interval, whose update is a Bayesian posterior costing exactly one bit for every law, and a unitary lift equips each law with an isospectral Sturm-Liouville system and a quantum carpet: a strong law of large numbers for the critical Sobolev mass, with sharp constant given by Wiener's jump statistic, proves the fractality of the Schrödinger density for arbitrary real bounded-variation data with a jump, previously known only for rational step data. A lift to the dual of the Virasoro algebra, at central charge one, a normalization, identifies laws with disconjugate Hill potentials, tails with conformal weights, and the Cauchy family with the exceptional orbit. Randomized phases yield an exact sampler and a multiplicative chaos, log-correlated in the weakly tempered regime; in several dimensions, dependence stratifies into a conserved interaction, a torsion driven by maximal correlation, and a flat Sinkhorn transport.

</details>


### [149] [The KLS constant is $O(\log^{1/4} n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24164)
*Brayden Letwin*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文确认了Kannan-Lovász-Simonovits（KLS）猜想在二次型情形下的成立，并利用该结果将KLS常数改进为ψ_n ≤ C log^{1/4} n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KLS猜想是高维凸几何与概率论中的重要猜想，但此前尚未在一般情形下被完全证明。本文旨在证明该猜想在二次型这一特殊但关键情形下的成立。

Method: 首先证明对于任意各向同性对数凹随机向量X，任给对称矩阵M，方差Var(⟨MX,X⟩) ≤ 2 𝔼|∇⟨MX,X⟩|²成立。然后取M为特定形式，将结果应用于KLS常数的估计。

Result: 成功证明KLS猜想对二次形成立，并且得到KLS常数上界ψ_n ≤ C log^{1/4} n。

Conclusion: 该结果为KLS猜想的一般性证明提供了重要进展，同时改进的KLS常数上界对高维几何与算法分析有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We confirm the Kannan--Lovász--Simonovits conjecture for quadratic forms: if $X \sim μ$ is an isotropic log-concave random vector in $\mathbb{R}^n$, then for any symmetric matrix $M$ one has $$ \operatorname{Var}_{X \sim μ}(\langle MX,X\rangle) \leq 2\,\mathbb{E}_{X \sim μ}|\nabla\langle MX,X\rangle|^2. $$ As an application, we apply the above to $M=\mathbb{E}_{X \sim μ}(\langle X,θ\rangle X\otimes X)$ for $θ\in S^{n-1}$ and show that the Kannan--Lovász--Simonovits constant $ψ_n$ satisfies $$ ψ_n\leq C\log^{1/4}n $$ for some absolute constant $C>0$.

</details>


### [150] [Strong rate of convergence for the Euler-Maruyama scheme of additive fractional SDEs with Lipschitz drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24166)
*Tsukasa Moritoki*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究了分数布朗运动驱动下加性随机微分方程的Euler-Maruyama格式强收敛率，利用移位随机缝合方法证明当Hurst参数H∈(1/2,1)时收敛率为1，H∈(0,1/2]时收敛率为1/2+H-ε。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对分数布朗运动驱动的随机微分方程，现有数值方法的收敛性分析尚不完善，特别是对Hurst参数全范围(0,1)的强收敛率需要系统研究。

Method: 采用移位随机缝合论证方法，利用分数布朗运动的条件高斯结构来控制噪声离散化误差，对Euler-Maruyama格式进行误差分析。

Result: 当H∈(1/2,1)时，强收敛率达到1；当H∈(0,1/2]时，强收敛率为1/2+H-ε（对任意ε>0）。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法有效刻画了分数布朗运动驱动下随机微分方程数值解的收敛速度，为全范围Hurst参数提供了明确的收敛率界。

Abstract: We study the strong convergence rate of the Euler-Maruyama scheme for additive stochastic differential equations driven by a fractional Brownian motion with Hurst parameter $H \in (0,1)$. Assuming the drift coefficient to be Lipschitz continuous, we show that the rate is $1$ if $H \in (1/2,1)$, and $1/2+H-\varepsilon$, for any $\varepsilon>0$, if $H \in (0,1/2]$. The main ingredient is a shifted stochastic sewing argument, which exploits the conditional Gaussian structure of fractional Brownian motion to control the noise discretization error.

</details>


### [151] [On non-monotonicity of logarithmic energy for random matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24170)
*Theodoros Assiotis*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文构造了有限能量Wigner反例和参数化高斯正则化伯努利入口律，反驳了Chafaï等人关于二次惩罚对数能量维度单调性的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Chafaï、Dadoun和Youssef提出的关于平均经验谱分布的二次惩罚对数能量维度单调性猜想，本文试图构造反例来证伪该猜想。

Method: 通过构造有限能量Wigner反例，以及一个具体的单参数族高斯正则化伯努利入口律，作为反驳猜想的手段。

Result: 成功构造了反例，表明二次惩罚对数能量的维度单调性猜想不成立。

Conclusion: Chafaï等人提出的二次惩罚对数能量维度单调性猜想被证伪。

Abstract: We construct a finite-energy Wigner counterexample and a concrete one-parameter family of Gaussian-regularised Bernoulli entry laws yielding counterexamples to the conjectural dimensional monotonicity of the quadratically penalised logarithmic energy for mean empirical spectral distributions by Chafaï, Dadoun, and Youssef.

</details>


### [152] [On Feige's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24528)
*Zipei Nie,Jiaye Wei*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文给出了Feige猜想的简短证明：对于n个期望为1的独立非负随机变量，其和小于n+1的概率至少为(n/(n+1))^n ≥ 1/e。该证明借助GPT-5.6 Sol完成，并基于Vlassis和Thomas关于Gaffke猜想（无分布p值的有限样本有效性）的突破性工作，同时讨论了后续相关工作的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明Feige猜想，该猜想涉及独立非负随机变量和的下界概率问题，是概率论与统计学中的一个重要猜想。

Method: 借助GPT-5.6 Sol辅助推导，基于Vlassis和Thomas关于Gaffke猜想（无分布p值的有限样本有效性）的突破性结果，构建简短证明。

Result: 成功给出了Feige猜想的简短证明：P(∑X_i < n+1) ≥ (n/(n+1))^n ≥ 1/e。

Conclusion: 该证明为Feige猜想提供了简洁的数学验证，并指出后续工作（Ming, Ramdas, Shen, Wang, Waudby-Smith）的相关意义。

Abstract: We present a short proof of Feige's conjecture: for $n$ independent nonnegative random variables with expectation one, the probability that their sum is less than $n+1$ is at least $\left(\frac{n}{n+1}\right)^n\ge \frac{1}{e}$. The proof was obtained with the assistance of GPT-5.6 Sol and builds on the recent breakthrough of Vlassis and Thomas establishing Gaffke's conjecture on the finite-sample validity of a distribution-free $p$-value. We also discuss the implications of the subsequent work of Ming, Ramdas, Shen, Wang, and Waudby-Smith.

</details>


### [153] [Colored Markov-Modulated Brownian Motion for Preemptive Workload Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24533)
*Maria Laura Battagliola,Eduardo Medina-Valdés,Oscar Peralta*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种彩色马尔可夫调制布朗运动模型，用于处理新工作抢占活跃层、占据有序堆栈顶部、且需耗尽后才能恢复挂起工作的扩散尺度系统，并得到了平稳乘积形式表示和矩阵解析公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为扩散尺度系统中存在工作抢占（如后到先服务队列和中断驱动调度）的场景提供数学建模框架，其中处理速率本身会随机波动。

Method: 通过颜色编码堆栈位置和优先级，背景相位通过第一类转换控制漂移和波动率，第二类转换在严格正随机启动高度创建更高颜色，利用后向递归通过返回矩阵和占据密度核总结高颜色游程，并在hold-and-jump和regulated-base边界约定下推导平稳乘积形式。

Result: 得到了平稳乘积形式的表示，给出了显式的矩阵解析公式，并为典型的启动高度族提供了矩阵变换公式。

Conclusion: 该模型为具有抢占和随机处理速率的扩散系统提供了理论分析工具，矩阵解析形式的解便于实际计算和应用。

Abstract: We develop a colored Markov-modulated Brownian motion model for diffusion-scale systems in which newly created work preempts the active layer, occupies the top of an ordered stack, and must deplete before suspended work resumes. Such preemption arises whenever new work interrupts whatever is currently active, as in last-come, first-served queues and interrupt-driven scheduling, with diffusive dynamics appropriate when the processing rate itself fluctuates randomly. Colors encode stack position and priority, while a background phase, evolving through first-kind transitions that leave the active color unchanged, controls drift and volatility within each color. Second-kind transitions create higher colors at strictly positive random launch heights. A backward recursion summarizes higher-color excursions through return matrices and occupation-density kernels. We obtain stationary product-form representations under hold-and-jump and regulated-base boundary conventions, given explicitly through matrix-analytic formulae, and give matrix-transform formulas for illustrative launch-height families.

</details>


### [154] [Range convergence and sharp one-arm asymptotics for the critical percolation cluster in high dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24561)
*M. Cabezas,D. Croydon,A. Fribergh,N. Kawamoto*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文证明了高维临界伯努利键渗流模型中，原点团簇的重新标度经验测度收敛于超布朗运动的总占据测度，并结合一致下界得到团簇作为紧集在Hausdorff度量下的收敛性，进而推导出精确的单臂渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示高维临界渗流模型中团簇的宏观尺度极限行为，特别是其与超布朗运动的深刻联系，并改进已有关于单臂概率的渐近结果。

Method: 通过证明重新标度经验测度在σ-有限意义下收敛到超布朗运动的总占据测度，结合关于临界团簇相对于外度规的一致质量下界，再利用Hausdorff度量下紧集收敛性推导单臂渐近。

Result: 证明了r^2 P(0↔∂B_r) → θ_1 ∈ (0,∞)，即单臂概率的精确二阶渐近，改进了Kozma和Nachmias的结果。

Conclusion: 高维临界渗流团簇的极限结构由超布朗运动刻画，并由此导出了精细的几何渐近性质。

Abstract: For critical Bernoulli bond percolation on $\Z^d$ in the high-dimensional regime, we prove that the rescaled empirical measure of the cluster of the origin converges, in a suitable $σ$-finite sense, to the total occupation measure of super-Brownian motion. Combined with a uniform lower mass bound for the critical cluster with respect to the extrinsic (Euclidean) metric, which we also prove and which is of independent interest, the measure convergence further yields the convergence of the rescaled cluster as a compact set, in the Hausdorff metric. As a consequence, we are able to obtain the sharp one-arm asymptotics $r^2\,\bP(0\leftrightarrow \partial B_r)\to θ_1\in(0,\infty)$, hence refining a result of Kozma and Nachmias.

</details>


### [155] [The Yang-Mills measure on surfaces via Morse theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24640)
*Reda Chhaibi,Nguyen Viet Dang,Yannick Guedes Bonthonneau,Gabriel Rivière,Tat Dat Tô*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于莫尔斯理论的杨-米尔斯测度构造方法，通过在紧致黎曼曲面上的联络空间中直接构建连续版本的测度，解决了此前仅能通过格点逼近实现的问题。该方法引入了莫尔斯规范概念，并解决了与莫尔斯-斯梅尔向量场相关的随机上同调方程，最终恢复了米格达尔、威滕和莱维公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前杨-米尔斯测度仅能通过格点逼近（如Chevyrev在平坦环面情形及作者与Nohra在一般紧致黎曼曲面情形）获得，缺乏直接的连续构造方法。作者希望建立一种不依赖离散化的连续理论框架。

Method: 1. 提出"莫尔斯规范"新概念；2. 解决与莫尔斯-斯梅尔向量场相关的随机上同调方程（通过改进莫尔斯-斯梅尔梯度流的指数收敛到平衡的结果）；3. 将随机解与莫尔斯复形数据结合，引入自由杨-米尔斯测度；4. 利用随机微分方程工具定义随机联络沿曲线的和乐；5. 通过适当条件化定义杨-米尔斯测度。

Result: 成功构造了紧致黎曼曲面上联络空间的连续版本杨-米尔斯测度；计算了配分函数及随机和乐的分布律；恢复了Migdal、Witten和Lévy公式。

Conclusion: 莫尔斯理论为杨-米尔斯测度的连续构造提供了有效途径，该方法统一并推广了此前格点逼近的结果，且得到与已知物理公式一致的理论成果。

Abstract: We introduce a Morse theoretical approach to the construction of the Yang--Mills measure on the space of connections of a compact Riemannian surface. This provides a direct continuous version of this measure which was previously obtained through lattice approximations by Chevyrev in the case of the flat torus and by one of the authors and Nohra for general compact Riemannian surfaces.
  The starting point is the new notion of a Morse gauge together with the resolution of random cohomological equations associated to Morse--Smale vector fields. This is achieved by improving exponential convergence to equilibrium results for Morse--Smale gradient flows that were obtained by two of the authors in the context of the study of Ruelle spectra and by Jia, Stewart and Sverak in the context of simplified models from fluid mechanics. Combining these random solutions with the data given by the Morse complex, we introduce a free Yang-Mills measure on space of connections and, using classical tools from stochastic differential equations, we show how to make sense of holonomies for random connections along a large class of curves.
  Finally, by setting a proper conditioning of this free measure through these random holonomies, we define the Yang--Mills measure and we compute its partition function together with the law of random holonomies with respect to this measure, recovering the formulas from the works of Migdal, Witten and Lévy.

</details>


### [156] [Majority Dynamics on Assortative Sparse Stochastic Block Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24652)
*Ioana Dumitriu,Muchen Ju,Hai-Xiao Wang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在可重采样稀疏二元随机块模型上的多数动力学过程，发现加权优势 Δ̃_t = b|B_t| - a|R_t| 而非简单优势 Δ_t 主导了达成一致意见的速度，并识别了蓝色意见达成一致所需的三个时间区段（常数时间、次多项式时间、多项式时间），同时给出了相应的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在社团结构网络中多数意见动态的收敛速度，特别是当网络结构随意见变化而动态重采样时，意见传播的动力学行为。

Method: 采用稀疏二元随机块模型（assortative regime），图根据当前意见划分动态重采样；定义加权优势量 Δ̃_t；通过单顶点翻转概率的细致估计（稀疏二项分布差值）进行分析，并结合大偏差理论推导收敛时间。

Result: 识别出三个收敛时间区段：1) 当 Δ̃_0 ≳ -N/√log N 时，3步内达成一致；2) 当 Δ̃_0 < 0 且 |Δ̃_0| = o(N) 时，N^{o(1)} 步内达成一致；3) 当 Δ̃_0 < 0, |Δ̃_0| = O(N) 且 Δ_0 ≫ √(N/log N) 时，N^{I_0+o(1)} 步内达成一致（I_0 由ReLU函数定义）。反之，当 |B_0|/|R_0| ≤ a/b - κ 且 Δ_0 > 0 时，至少需要 N^{I_0 - o(1)} 步。

Conclusion: 加权优势量比简单数量优势更准确地刻画了动态重采样网络中的意见收敛速度；不同参数区间对应截然不同的收敛时间尺度，揭示了网络结构与意见动态之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Majority dynamics is a two-opinion process in which each vertex repeatedly updates to the majority opinion among its neighbors. We study this process on a resampled sparse binary stochastic block model in the assortative regime. At each time step, a graph is sampled from the current opinion partition: vertices with the same opinion are joined with probability $α=a\log N/N$, while vertices with differing opinions are joined with probability $β=b\log N/N$, where $a>b>1$. Let $B_t$ and $R_t$ denote the blue and red camps at time $t$. We show that the weighted advantage $\widetildeΔ_t =b|B_t|-a|R_t|$, rather than the unweighted advantage $Δ_t=|B_t|-|R_t|$ alone, governs the pace to unanimity.
  Our results, which hold with high probability as \(N\to\infty\), identify three regimes for blue unanimity under the initial blue advantage, i.e., $Δ_0>0$: constant time, subpolynomial time, and polynomial time. First, when $\widetildeΔ_0 \gtrsim -N/\sqrt{\log N}$, blue unanimity occurs within three updates. Second, when $\widetildeΔ_0 < 0$ and $|\widetildeΔ_0| = o(N)$, blue unanimity occurs within $N^{o(1)}$ updates. Furthermore, when $\widetildeΔ_0 < 0$, $|\widetildeΔ_0| = O(N)$, and $Δ_0\gg\sqrt{N/\log N}$, blue unanimity still occurs within $N^{I_0+o(1)}$ updates, where \[ I_0= \left(\mathbf{ReLU}\Big(\sqrt{a\frac{|R_0|}{N}}-\sqrt{b\frac{|B_0|}{N}}\Big)\right)^2, \] and $\mathbf{ReLU}(x)=\max\{x,0\}$. Conversely, away from the weighted threshold, when $|B_0|/|R_0|\le a/b-κ$ and $Δ_0>0$, $N^{I_0 - o(1)}$ updates are necessary for blue unanimity. Our analysis relies on detailed estimates for one-vertex flip probabilities in sparse binomial differences, which could be of independent interest.

</details>


### [157] [Spectral aspects of random heavy-tailed tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24695)
*Remi Bonnin,Alexis Imbert*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究了重维格纳张量（heavy-Wigner tensors）的渐近性质，证明了在适当归一化后仅有肥超树（fat hypertrees）贡献非零渐近项，并建立了注射迹不变量（injective trace invariants）的中心极限定理。作为应用，分析了稀疏 Erdős-Rényi 超图的局部弱收敛性和邻接张量收缩后矩阵的谱分布，得到半圆律和依赖于稀疏参数的极限分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机张量（尤其是稀疏随机张量）的渐近行为，填补了重尾张量模型和稀疏超图邻接张量理论分析的空白，为理解高维稀疏数据的结构提供理论基础。

Method: 采用迹不变量（trace invariants）作为完全多项式不变量，结合组合图论中的肥超树（fat hypertrees）概念，运用矩方法和中心极限定理进行渐近分析。对 Erdős-Rényi 超图应用局部弱收敛到 Galton-Watson 超树。

Result: 唯一非零渐近贡献来自肥超树；建立了注射迹不变量的中心极限定理。稀疏超图的邻接张量收缩后矩阵的谱分布：稀疏时依赖于参数 c 且支撑无界，稠密时收敛到半圆律。给出了任意重维格纳张量收缩矩阵谱测度矩的显式公式。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了重维格纳张量的渐近理论，将经典 Wigner 矩阵和随机矩阵谱理论推广到高阶张量情景，为稀疏超图分析提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We investigate heavy-Wigner tensors: symmetric random tensors whose independent entries, up to the tensor symmetries, are centered and have moments of order $N^{-(p-1)}$, where $N$ is the tensor dimension. This framework includes normalized adjacency tensors of sparse Erdős-Rényi hypergraphs and truncated heavy-tailed tensor models. We study trace invariants, a complete family of polynomial invariants under permutations of the tensor indices. We prove that, after the natural normalization, the only non-vanishing asymptotic contributions are those associated with fat hypertrees, and we derive a central limit theorem for these injective trace invariants.
  As applications, we first analyze Erdős-Rényi $p$-uniform hypergraphs with edge probability $α_N=c/N^{p-1}$. We prove local weak convergence to a uniform Galton-Watson hypertree with Poisson offspring distribution. We also prove convergence of the empirical spectral distribution of the matrix obtained by contracting the adjacency tensor; in the sparse regime the limiting law depends on the sparsity parameter $c$ and has unbounded support, while in the regime $N^{p-1}α_N\to\infty$ with $N^{p-1}(1-α_N)\to\infty$, the limiting spectral distribution is the semicircle law. This result generalizes for matrices obtained by contracting an arbitrary heavy-Wigner tensor and we derive an explicit formula for the moments of the limiting spectral measure.

</details>
